<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/margaret-hamilton-computing-pioneer-dies-1007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Margaret Hamilton has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died. Her work helped formalize software engineering as a discipline and improve the reliability of critical flight systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。她的去世让人们回顾这位女性在早期计算领域的重大贡献及其对现代软件的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/margaret-hamilton-portrait-toan-trinh-mit-technology-review-A1_1.png' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/margaret-hamilton-portrait-toan-trinh-mit-technology-review-A1_1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2018</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility. The release underscores the momentum behind community-driven models as alternatives to big incumbents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。此举反映了开源模型在市场上的持续崛起，成为对抗大型垄断模型的有力选项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare acquires Deno</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 收购 Deno，整合边缘计算生态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 711</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform. The deal could accelerate edge-based development and broaden Cloudflare’s developer toolkit, though it raises questions about governance and open-source leadership in the workspace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。此举可能加速基于边缘的开发并扩展 Cloudflare 的开发者工具包，同时也引发关于治理与开源领导力的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare/deno-cf-balanced-og.webp' data-full='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare/deno-cf-balanced-og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 886</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful project turns a house illustration into a Home Assistant dashboard. It blends art with automation, illustrating how visual design can improve smart-home usability and engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个有趣的尝试：把房子插画直接做成 Home Assistant 的仪表板。通过将艺术设计引入智能家居界面，展示了美观与易用性并重的积极效果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49949438' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Bob Cringely has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲍勃·克林西利去世（Bob Cringely）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 861</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49953495' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 853</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Florida woman used Anthropic&#x27;s Claude to draft a diary entry and then reported it to police, sparking questions about the role of AI-generated content in investigations and potential legal implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名女子使用 Anthropic 的日记功能草拟条目后向警方报案，此事引发对 AI 生成内容在调查中作用及潜在法律影响的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 764</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components. It highlights the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and feature completeness on future macOS builds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。此举揭示了系统级 AI 功能对存储与性能的影响，以及未来 macOS 构建中的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rea.tools/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>REA Reverse – Engineer Anything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>REA Reverse：逆向工程万物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 755</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work. It aims to democratize RE skills by curating practical content and community-driven exercises. This matters for defenders, researchers, and hardware hackers looking to deepen code comprehension and vulnerability analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。通过实操内容和社区驱动的练习，推动逆向工程技能的普及。对安全研究、漏洞分析和硬件黑客都具有现实意义。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 730</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I&#x27;ve funded a rural Tanzanian student&#x27;s education for 10 years, covering school fees and supplies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分享者表示自己资助一名坦桑尼亚农村学生教育十年，涵盖学费与学习用品等支出。该故事凸显长期资助的持续影响，并提出技术界同侪可复制或支持的低摩擦、可扩展的慈善模式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://htmx.org/essays/yes-and/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yes, and</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yes, and（是的，而且）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 719</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation. Using HTMX as a practical lens, it shows how to build incrementally while preserving compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。以 HTMX 为例，展示如何在保持向后兼容的同时实现逐步扩展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/youtuber-says-cops-paid-him-a-visit-after-he-built-flock-style-camera-to-track-cops-2000824306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTuber Says Cops Visited Him After He Built a Flock-Style Camera to Track Cops</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网红称警方在他自制的Flock风格摄像头追踪警察后上门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50026555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50026555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 356</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A YouTuber says police visited him after he built a Flock-style camera to track cops. The incident highlights tensions between civilian surveillance tools and law enforcement. It raises questions about privacy, safety, and accountability when DIY cameras monitor public spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>网红称警方在他自制的Flock风格摄像头用于追踪警察后上门。此事暴露了民用监控工具与执法之间的紧张关系，也引发对隐私和安全的讨论。它强调在 DIY 摄像头监控公共场域时，谁应对行为负责以及相关法律边界的问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oxide.computer/blog/our-445m-series-d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our $445M Series D</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们完成4.45亿美元的D轮融资</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 678</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oxide Computer announced a $445M Series D round, fueling its cloud-native hardware platform designed for disaggregated data centers. The tranche signals sustained investor interest in software-defined infrastructure and enterprise-grade edge/cloud deployments, while boosting R&amp;D and go-to-market efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oxide Computer 宣布完成4.45亿美元的 D 轮融资，为其面向云原生硬件平台的去中心化数据中心设计提供资金。此轮融资反映投资者对软件定义基础设施与企业级边缘/云部署的持续兴趣，同时促进研发与市场拓展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49994443' data-tags='Show HN,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bigwords.page/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bigwords.page – Turn any screen into a sign. The URL is the app</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bigwords.page：把任意屏幕变成标牌，URL 就是应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bigwords.page turns any URL into a shareable oversized sign displayed on a screen, effectively turning the URL itself into the app. It&#x27;s a lightweight, browser-based signage tool suitable for events or quick alerts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bigwords.page 把任意链接变成可在屏幕上显示的超大标牌，URL 本身即为应用。这个轻量的浏览器工具适合活动现场或快速发布通知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bigwords.page/og.png' data-full='https://bigwords.page/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117395269325940185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Math 2.0” will need to value mathematical progress more holistically</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“Math 2.0”将以更全面的标准评估数学进步</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 638</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams. It emphasizes holistic indicators such as conceptual understanding, problem-solving, collaboration, and real-world impact, which could reshape curricula and assessment practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。它强调概念理解、问题解决、协作与实际应用等全面指标，认为这将影响课程设计与评估方式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2D Vehicles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2D 车辆系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction. It emphasizes balancing realism with a lightweight engine, showing how small tweaks can dramatically affect handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。作者平衡真实感与简化，展示微小物理参数的调整如何影响轻量级引擎中的操控性与性能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.helgilibrary.com/companies/jetbrains' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JetBrains reports revenue growth, net financial loss for 2025</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JetBrains 报告 2025 年收入增长，但净亏损</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 590</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JetBrains posted revenue growth for 2025 but finished the year with a net loss. The results reflect heavy investments in product development and expansion, indicating profitability remains a longer-term milestone even as top-line growth accelerates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年收入有所增长，但净亏损仍然出现。公司正加大产品开发与市场扩张的投入，短期利润承压，但长期的订阅生态和市场份额潜力仍被看好。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.classaction.org/news/visa-mastercard-major-banks-facing-new-litigation-over-anticompetitive-merchant-credit-card-transaction-fees' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Visa, Mastercard, major banks facing new litigation over &#x27;anticompetitive&#x27; fees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Visa、Mastercard 与主要银行面临就“反竞争”手续费的新诉讼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New litigation accuses Visa, Mastercard, and major banks of anticompetitive merchant fees that raise costs for merchants and consumers. The case adds to regulatory scrutiny of card networks and could push fee structures toward reform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新诉讼指控 Visa、Mastercard 与多家银行联手设定反竞争的商户手续费，抬高商家和消费者成本。此案加剧监管对支付网络的关注，未来可能推动费率结构改革或和解结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.classaction.org/media/twitter-newswire-default.png' data-full='https://www.classaction.org/media/twitter-newswire-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare released a Web Search API that lets developers integrate search capabilities into apps with Cloudflare&#x27;s indexing, safety filters, and ranking. It signals a shift toward programmatic, privacy-conscious search experiences for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 公布了网页搜索 API，开发者可以在应用中集成搜索功能，利用 Cloudflare 的索引、安全过滤和排序。此举推动了面向开发者的可编程、更具隐私保护的搜索体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/physics/2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobel Prize in Physics 2026: Francis Halzen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026年诺贝尔物理学奖：弗朗西斯·哈尔岑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 567</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/1-2_3-6ac4a172d1e22-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/1-2_3-6ac4a172d1e22-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web. JPEG XL offers higher quality at smaller file sizes and supports features like animation and HDR, which could reduce bandwidth and streamline asset pipelines for publishers and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。JPEG XL 在更小尺寸下提供更高画质，并支持动画与 HDR，有望降低带宽、简化资产工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: GitHub refuses to remove cracked copies of my software after a month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 拒绝在一个月后移除我的破解软件副本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. The piece underscores the ongoing challenge creators face in maintaining control over distributed software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。文章强调创作者在分发软件时维权的持续挑战。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49971846' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT is adding real cartoonists&#x27; signatures to fake New Yorker cartoons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 将真实漫画家的签名添加到假冒的《纽约客》漫画中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur. This development underscores the challenges of provenance in AI-generated art and the potential need for watermarking or policy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Brendan-Loper-cartoon-700x394.jpg' data-full='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Brendan-Loper-cartoon-700x394.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.yandex.cloud/en/incidents/2136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3rd Yandex Cloud Data Center Was Hit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yandex 云数据中心再度受击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 549</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 667</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments. The incident tests customers&#x27; expectations for availability and the provider&#x27;s incident response and redundancy across sites. Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' data-full='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49969183' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reflection.ai/blog/introducing-beam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beam: Reflection&#x27;s 501B open-weight model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Beam：Reflection 的 501B 开放权重模型发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 549</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways. This expands access and invites community evaluation, with emphasis on safety and alignment considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。开放权重将推动研究者协同评估，并强调安全与对齐的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/sp40emik/production/771cfe201a3d24d7a340c3372e6a1654ec1a6e42-1800x1013.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/sp40emik/production/771cfe201a3d24d7a340c3372e6a1654ec1a6e42-1800x1013.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.1011now.com/2026/09/30/more-questions-than-answers-about-lincolns-google-data-center-water-electricity-usage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improper redaction reveals Google Data Center water and electricity usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>涂改错误暴露谷歌数据中心用水与用电数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 695</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>涂改错误意外暴露了谷歌数据中心的用水与用电数据，引发对超大规模基础设施能源披露与透明度的质疑。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://housing.over.pizza/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A city-building game in which the city would prefer you didn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个城市似乎不想让你成功的城市建造游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development. Players must navigate red tape, budget constraints, and unpopular policies, turning urban planning into a tense strategy challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。玩家需应对繁文缭节的行政流程、预算约束和不受欢迎的政策，使城市规划成为紧张的策略对决。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50021066' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/us-imposes-sanctions-international-criminal-court-hours-after-former-judge-wins-2026-10-09/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US imposes sanctions on ICC hours after former judge wins Nobel Peace Prize</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国在前任法官获诺贝尔奖后数小时对 ICC 实施制裁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hours after a former ICC judge was awarded the Nobel Peace Prize, the US announced sanctions on the International Criminal Court. The move underscores ongoing tensions between Washington and the ICC over investigations into powerful interests, potentially chilling cooperation and complicating future accountability efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在前 ICC 法官获得诺贝尔和平奖后数小时，美国宣布对国际刑事法院实施制裁。此举凸显华盛顿与 ICC 之间持续的紧张关系，可能削弱合作并使未来追责工作变得更为复杂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50032556' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carette.xyz/posts/lobbying_and_corruption/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lobbying is corruption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>游说就是腐败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lobbying concentrates influence among moneyed interests, which can distort policy and undermine fair representation. The piece argues that this dynamic amounts to corruption and calls for stronger transparency and anti-corruption reforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>游说把影响力集中在有钱势力手中，侵蚀平等的政治代表性。文章指出这一现象促成腐败并扭曲政策，应加强透明度与反腐改革。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49962012' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cpr.dk/cpr-nyt/nyhedsarkiv/2026/okt/omfattende-uautoriseret-adgang-til-borgeres-cpr-oplysninger' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denmark data breach exposes 8.8M people&#x27;s personal data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦数据泄露曝光880万公民个人信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49962012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49962012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large Danish data breach exposed personal data for about 8.8 million people due to unauthorized access. The incident underscores government data security risks and the need for stronger access controls, auditing, and breach response measures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>未经授权的访问导致约880万丹麦公民的个人信息被曝光（包括 CPR 编号等）。此事件凸显政府数据安全的脆弱性，强调需要加强访问控制、审计与事件响应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn9.cpr.dk/cpr/media/12018/cpr-logo-440px.png' data-full='https://cdn9.cpr.dk/cpr/media/12018/cpr-logo-440px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49970667' data-tags='AI,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vals.ai/blogs/room-temperature-magnetic-semiconductors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Opus 5.5 agents discover two room-temperature magnetic semiconductor candidates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Opus 5.5 的代理发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49970667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49970667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI agents in the Opus framework autonomously discovered two candidate room-temperature magnetic semiconductors, pushing spintronics closer to practical devices. This showcases how AI-assisted materials discovery can accelerate breakthroughs that once seemed out of reach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Opus 框架中的 AI 代理自动发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料，为自旋电子学向实用器件推进带来希望。这体现了 AI 辅助的材料发现能显著加速原本难以实现的突破。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vals.ai/blogs/2026-10-04-room-temperature-magnetic-semiconductors/hero.png' data-full='https://www.vals.ai/blogs/2026-10-04-room-temperature-magnetic-semiconductors/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50018420' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/peace/2026/press-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobel Peace Prize for 2026 to Navanethem Pillay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年诺贝尔和平奖授予 Navanethem &#x27;NAVI&#x27; Pillay</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 475</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Navanethem Pillay has been named the recipient of the 2026 Nobel Peace Prize, recognizing her decades of advocacy for human rights and international justice. The award underscores the ongoing emphasis on human rights as a foundation of global governance and diplomacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纳瓦涅坦·皮莱被授予2026年诺贝尔和平奖，表彰她多年来在维护人权和推动国际司法方面的努力。此项荣誉凸显人权在全球治理与外交中的持续重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/2026_peace_carrier_image_3-2-1024x683.png' data-full='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/2026_peace_carrier_image_3-2-1024x683.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49980880' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/grieving-the-loss-of-details/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grieving the loss of details</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>失去细节的哀悼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post laments how rapid tooling, AI-generated outputs, and abstraction layers erode granular details we rely on to debug and reproduce systems. It argues for preserving provenance and explainability to avoid opaque failures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者感叹快速工具、AI 生成输出和抽象层侵蚀了我们用于调试和复现系统的细节。文中呼吁保留来源可追溯性与可解释性，以避免日后难以追踪的故障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/grieving-the-loss-of-details/og.png' data-full='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/grieving-the-loss-of-details/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49977177' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polars 2.0 brings major performance and API improvements to the fast DataFrame library, with enhancements to lazy evaluation, memory usage, and language bindings. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polars 2.0 为高性能 DataFrame 库带来重大的性能与 API 改进，提升延迟求值、内存利用率与语言绑定。此版本增强了 Python 与 Rust 的数据处理工作流的速度与可扩展性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50029123' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://beaksec.github.io/posts/telegram-desktop-one-click-account-takeover/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Telegram Desktop vulnerability allowed any user&#x27;s file to be stolen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Telegram 桌面端漏洞致使任意用户文件可能被窃取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Telegram Desktop suffered a vulnerability that let an attacker access and steal a victim&#x27;s files via the desktop client, potentially enabling a one-click account takeover. The bug highlights how trusted desktop apps can become backdoors for data exfiltration, so users should update to patched versions and developers should harden local storage and permission handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Telegram 桌面端存在漏洞，攻击者可通过桌面客户端访问并窃取受害者的文件，甚至实现一键式账户接管。这一漏洞凸显受信任的桌面应用可能成为数据外泄的后门，用户应尽快升级打补丁，开发者需加强本地存储与权限管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beaksec.github.io/assets/img/posts/telegram-desktop-one-click-account-takeover/cover.png' data-full='https://beaksec.github.io/assets/img/posts/telegram-desktop-one-click-account-takeover/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a practical framework for building a personal decision model to navigate uncertainty, balancing probabilities, utilities, and decision rules. The piece provides templates and steps to tailor the model across domains, helping maintain consistency in high-stakes choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提供一个实用框架，帮助你在不确定情境中构建个人决策模型，结合概率、效用与决策规则。文中给出可定制的模板和步骤，便于在不同场景应用，提升关键决策的一致性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TypeSafe AI raised $870 million at a $7.5 billion valuation, underscoring investor enthusiasm for safety-focused AI tooling. The funding will likely accelerate product development, governance features, and enterprise adoption of safer AI systems.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/10/08/open-ai-revenue-nvidia-oracle-coreweave.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI annualised revenues $20B less than previously signalled</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 年化收入比此前预计低出200亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 427</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 300</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reported annualized revenue that came in about $20B below what investors had signaled, signaling a sharper slowdown or slower-than-expected monetization of its model. The shortfall highlights how even top-tier AI platforms are still iterating on pricing, enterprise adoption, and cost-to-revenue dynamics as competition and infrastructure costs mount.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的年化收入约比此前透露的数字低出约200亿美元。该修正透露出其商业化路径的不确定性，以及企业客户的实际 uptake 以及竞争与基础设施成本对盈利的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nitter.net' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter: Update Oct 10th seeking funding and legal help</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nitter posted an Oct 10 update signaling fundraising and legal assistance to keep the project afloat amid potential legal pressure. The move highlights the fragility of privacy-focused, open-source social-frontends facing corporate enforcement. Funding and legal support are central to its survival prospects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nitter 在10月10日的一次更新中表示将寻求资金与法律援助，以应对潜在的法律压力而继续运营。此举凸显了面向隐私的开源社交前端在面对企业执法时的脆弱性。资金与法律支持成为其生存关键。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/embeddinggemma-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EmbeddingGemma 2: An open, lightweight multimodal embedding model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2：一个开源、轻量级的多模态嵌入模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EmbeddingGemma 2 builds on Google&#x27;s open Gemma line with a lightweight, multimodal embedding model designed for fast retrieval across text and vision. By staying open and small, it lowers barriers for researchers and developers to prototype cross-modal apps and run experiments on modest hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EmbeddingGemma 2 在开放的 Gemma 基础上，提供一个轻量级的多模态嵌入模型，面向快速跨模态检索。保持开源和体积小，降低研究者和开发者在中等硬件上原型化跨模态应用的门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50040276' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lineageos.org/Changelog-32/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LineageOS 24</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LineageOS 24.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LineageOS 24.0 marks a new release with updated Android base and refreshed privacy and customization features. It broadens device support and provides security improvements for users seeking open-source Android alternatives. The update continues LineageOS&#x27; momentum as a long-running custom ROM option.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LineageOS 24.0 发布，基于更新的 Android 版本并带来隐私与自定义功能的改进。它扩展了设备支持范围并为追求开源 Android 替代方案的用户提供安全性提升。此次更新延续了 LineageOS 长期持续发展的势头。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lineageos.org/images/2026-10-11/hero.webp' data-full='https://lineageos.org/images/2026-10-11/hero.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50018817' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/franzenzenhofer/big-arrow-on-the-screen' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Let your AI agents paint big arrows, boxes and text on your screen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 404</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time. Such overlays boost interpretability and debugging, helping humans understand where the agent is headed and why. The approach is easy to prototype and could be used in demos, tutorials, or human-in-the-loop workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。此类覆盖能提升可解释性与调试效果，帮助人类理解代理的意图与下一步动作。该方法易于原型化，适用于演示、教程和人机协作场景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50031269' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cphpost.dk/2026-10-10/news/round-up/123456-password-used-in-massive-danish-cpr-data-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>`123456&#x27; password used in Danish CPR data breach</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦 CPR 数据泄露中使用了 123456 密码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Attackers used the simple password 123456 to access Danish CPR records, exposing sensitive personal data. This incident shows how weak credentials can enable large scale government data breaches and underscores the need for stronger authentication and password hygiene across public-facing systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>攻击者使用简单密码 123456 访问了丹麦 CPR 记录，暴露了敏感个人信息。该事件凸显了弱密码如何使政府数据大规模泄露成为可能，也强调在面向公众的系统中需要更强的身份认证和密码管理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50004790' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quesma.com/blog/invisible-cities-one-shot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I gave Opus 5.5 one prompt and six hours to visualize Invisible Cities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>给 Opus 5.5 一个提示，花六小时可视化《隐形城市》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A single prompt and hours-long iteration to coax Opus 5.5 to visualize Italo Calvino&#x27;s &#x27;Invisible Cities&#x27; reveals how modern AI handles abstract literature. The experiment highlights both the model&#x27;s creative potential and its limits in mapping poetic descriptions to visuals, with time and prompt design driving the result.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用一个提示、持续数小时的迭代，试图让 Opus 5.5 将意大利作家卡维诺的《隐形城市》变为可视画面。结果揭示了当前多模态模型在将抽象文学转化为视觉表现方面的潜力与局限性，提示设计与时间投入对输出影响显著。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://quesma.com/_astro/thumbnail.CbJSiyQh.jpg' data-full='https://quesma.com/_astro/thumbnail.CbJSiyQh.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/szabadkai/c64-keyboard-font/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A font recreated from photographs of classic Commodore 64 keycaps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从经典 Commodore 64 键帽照片重现的字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design. It offers a retro aesthetic for UI or art and shows creative ways to derive glyph shapes from real-world objects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。为 UI/艺术提供复古美感，并展示了从真实物件派生字形的创意路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49997718' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=10169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mathocalypse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学灾变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 410</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scott Aaronson argues that AI and automated reasoning are transforming mathematical practice, challenging old assumptions about proofs and discovery. The piece reframes what counts as a mathematical result and why human intuition matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scott Aaronson 认为人工智能和自动推理正在改变数学研究的实践，挑战关于证明与发现的传统观念，重新定义何为有意义的数学成果，并强调人类直觉的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50011928' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychiatry/articles/10.3389/fpsyt.2025.1697900/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ADHD as a circadian rhythm disorder: evidence and implications for chronotherapy (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ADHD 被视为昼夜节律障碍：证据与时序治疗的启示（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50011928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50011928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence positions ADHD as a circadian rhythm disorder, with implications for chronotherapy strategies. Timing medications and light exposure could help align symptoms and improve outcomes, signaling a shift toward circadian-aware care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新证据将 ADHD 定位为昼夜节律障碍，可能改变时序治疗策略。通过优化药物时机和光照暴露，或能提升治疗效果，推动以昼夜节律为核心的治疗模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.frontiersin.org/api/ipx/w=1200&amp;f=png/https://www.frontiersin.org/files/Articles/1697900/fpsyt-16-1697900-HTML/image_m/fpsyt-16-1697900-g001.jpg' data-full='https://www.frontiersin.org/api/ipx/w=1200&amp;f=png/https://www.frontiersin.org/files/Articles/1697900/fpsyt-16-1697900-HTML/image_m/fpsyt-16-1697900-g001.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50033407' data-tags='Open Source,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.bitwarden.com/t/published-version-update-in-app-stores/102750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitwarden Dual License Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bitwarden 双许可证模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden introduces a dual-licensing model to balance openness with commercial viability, offering an AGPL-licensed community edition alongside a paid license for enterprise deployments. This approach aims to keep core software open while providing a compliant path for large organizations to deploy Bitwarden at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden 推出双许可证模式，在社区版采用 AGPL 的同时提供企业级付费许可，以兼顾开源透明度与商业可行性。此举旨在为大型组织的部署提供合规路径，同时确保核心开源工作继续受益于社区贡献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49984025' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/decisions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decisions API is in public beta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decisions API 已进入公开测试版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decisions API is in public beta, enabling developers to encode multi-step decision making into apps using the model’s planning and recommendation capabilities. It helps build tools that surface options, trade-offs, and recommended actions with controllable constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decisions API 现处公开测试版，允许开发者在应用中嵌入多步决策能力，利用模型的规划与推荐功能输出选项、权衡与建议行动，并可对结果设定约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developers.openai.com/og/api/docs/guides/decisions.png' data-full='https://developers.openai.com/og/api/docs/guides/decisions.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/06/metas-muse-is-an-adorable-privacy-and-security-dumpster-fire/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s Muse is an adorable privacy and security dumpster fire</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s Muse is criticized for a privacy and security dumpster fire, with critics arguing the assistant mishandles data and raises risk. The article argues Muse&#x27;s safeguards are insufficient for a product handling sensitive interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 Muse 的隐私与安全问题引发批评，质疑这类 AI 助手在数据处理上的风险与保护不足。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论聚焦在5美元 VPS 上能长期在线且值得维护的服务，分享轻量级、鲁棒的工作负载与监控方法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49997161' data-tags='Privacy,Business,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rswebsols.com/news/meta-and-microsoft-take-steps-to-reduce-employee-usage-of-claude-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta and Microsoft take steps to reduce employee usage of Claude AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 与微软采取措施减少员工使用 Claude AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta and Microsoft are implementing controls to limit internal use of Claude AI among employees. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta和微软正在实施措施，限制员工使用 Claude AI 的范围。此举凸显在企业环境中对数据安全、隐私和治理的日益关注，可能影响员工的实验与协作方式。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test. The results reveal ambiguities and blockers that real users encounter, highlighting gaps in how information is communicated. It offers practical lessons on writing clearer, testable onboarding docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者花钱让人尝试按他的 README 做事，将文档变成一次真实测试。结果暴露出真实用户在执行步骤时遇到的歧义和阻碍，凸显文档传达中的不足。为编写更清晰、可测试的上手文档提供了可操作的经验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AnyPS5: Port PS5 binaries to PC without emulation (87% system libraries mapped)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AnyPS5：在不使用模拟的情况下将 PS5 二进制移植到 PC（87% 系统库已映射）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation. The project showcases progress in cross-architecture porting but raises legal, licensing, and anti-tampering questions as it matures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。该项目展示了跨架构移植的进展，但随着成熟，也引发法律、授权和反篡改等方面的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8ae3d3f41f22bb542f765977ce963b6143520cba572b6894151a7437ce067666/boykopovar/AnyPS5' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8ae3d3f41f22bb542f765977ce963b6143520cba572b6894151a7437ce067666/boykopovar/AnyPS5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/08/fired-openai-safety-researchers-dispute-misconduct-claims-warn-of-chilling-effect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI fires three safety researchers for &quot;mishandling research information&quot;</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reportedly fired three safety researchers over alleged mishandling of research information, sparking disputes about the facts and the implications for safety work at the company. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 因疑似不当处理研究信息解雇了三名安全研究人员，引发关于事实与对公司安全工作的影响的争论。批评者担心此举可能抑制内部安全研究与讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ircv3.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IRCv3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IRCv3 规范</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ircv3.net/img/logo-forwhite.svg' data-full='https://ircv3.net/img/logo-forwhite.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/history.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI Withdraws 3 Math Papers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 撤回三篇数学论文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates. The withdrawals highlight ongoing scrutiny of rigor and reproducibility in AI-aligned mathematical research, and point to the importance of versioning and clear disclosures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。此举凸显了围绕 AI 相关数学研究的严格性与可复现性持续受关注，强调版本控制和明确披露的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49976823' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-10-nature-capacity-species-lost-vastly.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nature&#x27;s capacity to &#x27;bounce back&#x27; when species are lost is overestimated: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>物种灭绝后自然界的恢复能力被严重高估</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research shows nature&#x27;s capacity to recover after species loss is vastly overestimated; simulations reveal that ecosystems may undergo slow recovery or irreversible shifts, with cascading effects. The finding implies conservation strategies should prioritize preventing losses of keystone species and maintaining network robustness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究表明，自然界在物种流失后恢复的能力被严重高估；模拟显示生态系统可能缓慢恢复或发生不可逆转的转变，产生连锁效应。该发现提醒在保护策略中应优先防止关键物种流失，维护生态网络的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/ecosystem.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/ecosystem.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A modern rebuild of the classic RTS Total Annihilation is designed to run on current PCs with updated visuals and gameplay refinements. The project highlights how fan-led remasters keep classic RTS titles relevant, showcasing modern compatibility layers and community modding energy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一部经典RTS《Total Annihilation》的现代化重建，目标在现今PC上运行并带来更新的画质和用户体验。此类粉丝复刻展现了复古游戏的持续魅力，也反映了现代兼容层和模组生态如何让老游戏重获生机。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the practical keyboard layout and shortcut differences between Windows and macOS, including Ctrl vs Cmd, Alt vs Option, and function keys. The piece explains how these variances affect cross-platform workflows, tooling, and onboarding for developers and power users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比 Windows 与 macOS 的键盘布局与快捷键差异，重点包括 Ctrl 与 Cmd、Alt 与 Option，以及功能键的不同。分析这对跨平台工作流、开发工具和新手上手的影响，以及如何在设计中考虑这些差异。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new paper examines how Navier–Stokes equations are translated into discrete algorithms and points out potential pitfalls that can lead to inaccurate or unstable simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇论文探讨将 Navier–Stokes 方程转化为数值算法时可能出现的问题，揭示数值建模中的潜在误差与稳定性挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FeSens/openTPU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenTPU – An open-source AI accelerator, developed by AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenTPU：一个开源的AI加速器项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures. By inviting community contributions, it aims to democratize access to AI hardware and accelerate experimentation beyond vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。通过鼓励社区贡献，它旨在降低对厂商锁定的依赖，推动 AI 硬件的普及与创新。</p>
