<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nyc.gov/main/click-to-cancel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC&#x27;s &quot;Click to Cancel&quot; rule is now in effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市的“点选取消”规则现已生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. The policy pushes platforms to simplify cancellations and clarify terms, potentially reshaping consumer rights online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。这一规定可能改变数字服务的用户体验和合规成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://itsallaboutthebit.com/i-am-sorry-but-you-still-have-to-think/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m sorry, but you still have to think</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that human thinking remains essential even as AI tools proliferate. It stresses critical reasoning, architectural planning, and ethical considerations as core skills for developers and teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张即便有 AI 工具，个人仍需亲自动脑。强调批判性思维、系统设计与伦理考量是开发者和管理者不可缺少的能力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer reflects on switching from Deno to Node, citing ecosystem maturity, tooling, and compatibility as decisive factors. The piece offers practical takeaways for developers choosing a JavaScript runtime in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回顾从 Deno 转向 Node 的经历，强调生态成熟度、工具链与兼容性等因素的决定性作用。为开发者在生产环境中选择 JavaScript 运行时提供了实用的见解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rociiu/talorys' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Talorys – A self-hosted personal AI agent on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Talorys：在 Cloudflare 免费层自托管的个人 AI 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription. It demonstrates how individuals can deploy private AI tools at the edge, balancing privacy, latency, and cost while avoiding vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。它展示了个人在边缘部署私有 AI 工具的可能性，平衡隐私、低延迟和成本，同时降低对单一厂商的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/396ee88ed01d50b7199d14902d8529d3fc69a488b90d388440f969869f61378f/rociiu/talorys' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/396ee88ed01d50b7199d14902d8529d3fc69a488b90d388440f969869f61378f/rociiu/talorys' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cedardb.com/blog/sqldoom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We ported the original Doom to SQL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们将原版 Doom 移植到 SQL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 315</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful project porting the original Doom to SQL showcases expressing game logic within a relational database. It highlights the boundaries of SQL as a platform for non-database tasks and sparks discussion about creativity versus practicality in data-centric programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个有趣的项目把原版 Doom 移植到 SQL，展示在关系型数据库中实现游戏逻辑的可能性。它揭示了 SQL 作为非数据库任务平台的边界，并引发关于数据驱动编程中创造力与实用性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cedardb.com/blog/sqldoom/hero.jpg' data-full='https://cedardb.com/blog/sqldoom/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49975619' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gleam.run/news/gleam-doesnt-compile-to-erlang-source-anymore/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gleam doesn&#x27;t compile to Erlang source anymore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gleam 不再直接编译为 Erlang 源码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Gleam compiler will no longer emit Erlang source code; it now targets BEAM bytecode directly. This reduces dependency on Erlang source generation and streamlines toolchains, potentially improving interoperability with Elixir and Erlang projects, though it may alter existing code-generation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gleam 编译器不再输出 Erlang 源码，而改为直接编译成 BEAM 字节码，减少对 Erlang 源码生成的依赖，简化工具链，并提升与 Elixir/Erlang 项目的互操作性。此举可能影响现有的代码生成工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gleam.run/images/preview/v1.19.png' data-full='https://gleam.run/images/preview/v1.19.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/03/why-dont-more-developers-use-the-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why don&#x27;t more developers “use the platform”?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何没有更多开发者真正使用这个平台？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers often hesitate to use a platform despite its promises, due to fragmentation, weak tooling, or unclear incentives. The piece argues for a better developer experience, concrete migration paths, and compelling value to raise adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管平台具备潜力，开发者往往因为生态碎片化、工具不足或激励不足而不愿真正采用。文章提出应改善开发者体验、提供清晰的迁移路径，并创造更具吸引力的价值以提升接受度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nolanlawson.com/_astro/dragndrop.CW1aBe6Q_ZDDLsQ.webp' data-full='https://nolanlawson.com/_astro/dragndrop.CW1aBe6Q_ZDDLsQ.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new tool maps the flattest paths across San Francisco, useful for cyclists and pedestrians who want to minimize climbs. By leveraging elevation data and routing heuristics, it shows how terrain shapes everyday commutes and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新工具绘制旧金山最平坦的路线，适合希望减少爬坡的骑行和步行者。它结合高程数据与路线策略，揭示地形如何影响日常通勤与无障碍出行。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/slides/math-2-0-caltech-2026.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao: Math 2.0 [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 363</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eye of Sauron: Long-Range Hidden Spy Camera Detection describes techniques to detect covert cameras from a distance, boosting privacy in public or semi-public spaces. It covers the capabilities and limits of such methods and what this means for security toolkits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《索伦之眼：长距离隐蔽摄像头检测》介绍了从远距离发现隐蔽摄像头的方法，提高在公共或半公共场所的隐私防护能力，同时讨论方法的能力边界与局限，以及对安防工具箱的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.themoscowtimes.com/2026/10/02/nearly-200-people-under-observation-after-irkutsk-lab-worker-dies-from-plague-a93857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nearly 200 people under observation after Irkutsk lab worker dies from plague</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Irkutsk 实验室工人疑似死于鼠疫，近200人被观察</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nearly 200 people are under observation after a lab worker in Irkutsk died from plague, prompting health authorities to trace exposures. The outbreak reaction illustrates the ongoing sensitivity of infectious disease surveillance near research facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊尔库茨克一名实验室工人疑似死于鼠疫引发关注，近200人进入观察和暴露监测，触发公共卫生响应。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/docker/docker-agent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docker Agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docker 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docker Agent introduces a framework to run autonomous agents inside containers, enabling programmable automation and orchestration at the edge or in CI pipelines. It exemplifies a rising trend of runtime agents embedded in dev tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docker Agent 提供在容器内运行自治代理的框架，支持在边缘、CI 流水线等场景中的自动化编排。展示了把运行时代理集成到开发工具链的日益兴起的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e5cb6fddbc2208b94a9a4eb5070c9942420b7b50edfe5ba5f1bc08b4a47e7320/docker/docker-agent' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e5cb6fddbc2208b94a9a4eb5070c9942420b7b50edfe5ba5f1bc08b4a47e7320/docker/docker-agent' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15 launches with performance improvements, better error messages, and standard library enhancements. The release continues Python’s modernization, while marking deprecated features that developers should begin migrating away from.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15 正式发布，带来性能提升、错误信息改进与标准库增强。该版本延续了 Python 的现代化进程，同时标注了一些将被弃用的特性，开发者应尽早迁移。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49990470' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/chemistry/2026/press-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobel Prize in Chemistry 2026 to Henri B. Kagan and Kenso Soai</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年诺贝尔化学奖授予 Henri B. Kagan 与 Kenso Soai</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49990470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2026 Nobel Prize in Chemistry goes to Henri B. Kagan and Kenso Soai for foundational work in asymmetric synthesis and catalysis. Their breakthroughs enable more selective creation of chiral molecules, with implications for pharmaceuticals, materials, and fundamental chemistry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年诺贝尔化学奖授予 Henri B. Kagan 与 Kenso Soai，表彰其在不对称合成与催化方面的开创性工作。其突破有助于更高选择性地制造手性分子，影响药物、材料以及基础化学研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/fig_ke_26_3x2-1024x683.jpg' data-full='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/fig_ke_26_3x2-1024x683.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49973598' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vivienhenz.com/common-lisp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Common Lisp is now the best programming language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 Common Lisp 现在是最佳编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49973598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49973598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vivien Henz argues that Common Lisp&#x27;s macro system, homoiconicity, and minimal runtime yield unrivaled metaprogramming and rapid DSL creation. The piece contends Lisp&#x27;s simplicity on a philosophical level makes it exceptionally productive for long-lived, complex systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 Common Lisp 的宏机制、同构性和极小的运行时开销，使其在元编程和快速构建领域无出其右。作者主张 Lisp 的简洁性和长期可维护性使其在处理复杂、长期的软件系统时更具生产力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://animationobsessive.substack.com/p/wallace-and-gromit-90-alone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Wallace and Gromit,&#x27; 90% Alone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《华莱士与格罗米特》：90% 的工作由独自完成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that modern stop-motion animation projects can be largely solo endeavors, leveraging automation and modular workflows to produce a feature-like result with limited collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文探讨在现代定格动画创作中，创作过程往往由单人主导，通过自动化与模块化工作流程实现接近大片水准的产出，强调个人在创作中的核心作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于地球按生物量计主导物种的讨论，蚂蚁常被引用为总量超过人类，这引发对生态系统规模与数据边界的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.opengroup.org//openbrand/register/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple/macOS removed from official Unix registry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果/macOS 已从官方 Unix 注册中移除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 287</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s macOS has been quietly removed from the Open Group&#x27;s official Unix branding registry, indicating a branding shift rather than a software change. The move matters for developers and vendors who rely on Unix branding for portability and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的 macOS 已从开放组织的官方 Unix 品牌注册名单中移除，体现的是品牌层面的变化而非系统本身的改动。此举可能影响那些依赖 Unix 品牌认知来展示兼容性和可移植性的开发者与厂商。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.opengroup.org/openbrand/register/images/tog-horiz.png' data-full='https://www.opengroup.org/openbrand/register/images/tog-horiz.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/apple-and-a-hackers-future/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple and a hacker&#x27;s future</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果与黑客的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49962857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49962857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery analyzes how Apple’s platform choices will shape the future for hackers and developers, balancing security with openness and monetization. The piece weighs the tension between control and opportunity in Apple’s ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery 分析苹果在平台策略上的走向将如何影响黑客与开发者，权衡安全、开放性与商业化之间的关系，讨论在苹果生态系统中的控制与机会之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stratechery.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/apple-hacker-5.png' data-full='https://stratechery.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/apple-hacker-5.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49987076' data-tags='Open Source,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://strandsagents.com/blog/introducing-strands-decider/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Strands Decider 2B: a small, open-source, decision model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Strands Decider 2B：一个小巧的开源决策模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Strands Decider 2B is a compact, open-source decision model designed for lightweight agent architectures. It presents a modular, pluggable decision-making framework that lets researchers experiment with different strategies without heavy infrastructure. The takeaway is that it lowers barriers to building transparent, auditable AI agents and accelerates prototyping of decision policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Strands Decider 2B 是为轻量级智能体架构设计的紧凑开源决策模型。它提供模块化、可插拔的决策框架，便于研究者在不依赖繁重基础设施的情况下试验不同策略。其意义在于降低构建可解释、可审计的智能体决策系统的门槛，加速决策策略的原型开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://strandsagents.com/blog/og/introducing-strands-decider.png' data-full='https://strandsagents.com/blog/og/introducing-strands-decider.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 Claude Code 找到了一个没人知道的行星</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using Claude Code, the author claims to have found a planet nobody knew existed, illustrating how AI-assisted coding can aid scientific discovery.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49998066' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/oldest-living-land-animal-jonathan-the-tortoise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Jonathan&#x27; is the oldest land animal on Earth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乔纳森：地球上最年长的陆地动物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jonathan the tortoise is recognized as the oldest living land animal, with estimates placing him in the high 180s to 190s range. His longevity offers a rare data point for aging biology and a cultural symbol of resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乔纳森龟被公认为现存最年长的陆地动物，年龄约在190岁左右。它的长寿为衰老生物学提供珍贵数据点，同时成为关于生命长久与韧性的文化象征。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/jonathan.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/jonathan.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49970871' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qlabs.sh/research/dust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dust: Pretraining Transformers Without Backpropagation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dust：无需反向传播的 Transformer 预训练</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49970871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49970871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dust introduces a pretraining approach for Transformers that does not rely on backpropagation, exploring alternative optimization or local-target strategies. Early results suggest comparable performance with different training dynamics and potential efficiency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dust 提出一种在不依赖反向传播的情况下对 Transformer 进行预训练的方法，探索替代优化或局部目标策略。初步结果显示在训练动力学与效率方面可能带来不同的优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://qlabs.sh/research/dust/figures/x-method.gif' data-full='https://qlabs.sh/research/dust/figures/x-method.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/mosquitoes-are-a-choice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The technology to eradicate mosquito-borne disease exists</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>消灭蚊媒疾病的技术已经存在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that proven tools—such as Wolbachia-infected mosquitoes, gene drives, and sterile insect techniques—could dramatically reduce mosquito-borne diseases, with governance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为通过 Wolbachia 等技术、基因驱动和昆虫性诱惑等手段，完全消灭蚊媒疾病是可实现的，但需要妥善治理与公众参与。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/10/paramount-completes-111b-warner-merger-creating-skydance-behemoth/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paramount Skydance has completed its $111B merger with Warner Bros. Discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>派拉蒙-斯凯丹斯完成对华纳兄弟探索公司的1110亿美元合并</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 505</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paramount Skydance completed its $111B merger with Warner Bros. Discovery, creating a media behemoth with scale across studios, franchises, and streaming. The deal consolidates a vast library of IP and streaming assets, potentially shaping negotiating leverage with distributors and talent, while drawing regulatory attention from antitrust authorities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>派拉蒙-斯凯丹斯完成对华纳兄弟探索公司1110亿美元合并，形成覆盖大片 IP、工作室与流媒体的庞大整合体。此次并购将重塑电影、电视及内容分发领域的竞争格局，并可能引发监管机构的审查与行业对手的连锁反应。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032758' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032758' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 643</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It suggests reform ideas like reassessing how properties are taxed and shielding homeowners from sudden tax kicks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨房价上涨与税负之间的矛盾，强调把房产价值与税负拆离所需的政策权衡。提出如重新评估房产税基准、保护房主避免税额骤增等改革思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zohaib.cc/blog/smartest-claude-code-feature' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code’s suggested message feature: I think the real customer is the model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的建议消息功能：真正的客户其实是模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code’s suggested message feature reveals a shift in who guides the interaction: the model itself plays a central role in proposing messages, highlighting design trade-offs around prompting, alignment, and the user-model relationship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 的建议消息功能暴露出一个趋势：模型本身在引导对话，成为提出消息的核心。这折射出提示设计、对齐与用户-模型关系的取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wallhop.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: WallHop – 12ft.io is gone, so I built a replacement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：WallHop——12ft.io 下线后的替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50038634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50038634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WallHop is a demonstration project offering a replacement experience after 12ft.io shut down, focusing on rendering content from paywalled pages. It highlights the ongoing interest in accessible reading experiences and the technical approaches to content extraction and rendering. Takeaway: as paywall tooling evolves, developers explore alternatives, raising legal/ethical questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WallHop 是一个 Show HN 项目，旨在在 12ft.io 下线后提供替代阅读体验，聚焦从付费墙页面提取并呈现内容的能力。反映了对可访问阅读体验的持续关注以及内容提取的技术路径。要点是随着付费墙工具的发展，开发者在探索替代方案，同时引发法律与伦理讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/games/1009140/ram-shortage-intel-amd-ddr4-comeback' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The RAM shortage is bringing back DDR4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM短缺推动DDR4回潮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sustained RAM shortages are backstopping demand for DDR4 as new-generation modules lag supply. The shift affects hardware pricing, upgrade cycles, and AI workloads that still rely on DDR4 compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM短缺仍在推高DDR4需求，因为新一代内存供货滞后。价格、升级周期以及仍依赖DDR4的AI工作负载都将因此受影响。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49980346' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.dancohen.org/archive/the-slow-formation-of-durable-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Slow Formation of Durable Software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>耐用软件的缓慢形成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Durable software emerges from disciplined, long-horizon practices rather than quick hacks. The piece argues for slow refactoring, stable teams, and robust testing as core investments, plus architectural discipline that pays off over years. It reframes software longevity as an organizational and process problem, not just code quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>耐用软件不是一朝一夕的产物，而是来自对长期规划、稳定团队、持续重构和全面测试的坚持。文章将软件的可持续性视为组织与流程的问题，强调从架构到维护的持续投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/f57f48fc-aaca-4b67-b624-c13a1643b08a.jpg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/f57f48fc-aaca-4b67-b624-c13a1643b08a.jpg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>备份之道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Tao of Backup distills practical wisdom for data protection, balancing redundancy, frequency, and recoverability. It provides actionable guidelines for building reliable backup strategies that survive hardware failures and human error.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《备份之道》提炼了数据保护的实用智慧，平衡冗余、备份频率与可恢复性。给出构建可靠备份策略的可执行准则，帮助抵御硬件故障与人为错误。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50007519' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.solarpowerworldonline.com/2026/10/4-hour-battery-storage-is-cheaper-to-install-than-gas-turbines-all-across-globe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4-hour battery storage is cheaper to install than gas turbines all across globe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四小时电池储能全球成本低于燃气涡轮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New analysis shows 4-hour battery storage is cheaper to install than gas turbines worldwide, signaling a shift in power system economics toward longer-duration storage. The result could accelerate grid decarbonization and influence utility planning and project development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球范围内研究显示四小时级别的电池储能安装成本低于燃气涡轮，标志着电力系统经济学向更长时间的储能转变。这可能推动电网脱碳、影响公用事业的规划与项目开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.solarpowerworldonline.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/stock-Holy-Cross-Energy-High-Mesa-Solar-Storage-site-in-Parachute-Colorado.jpg' data-full='https://www.solarpowerworldonline.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/stock-Holy-Cross-Energy-High-Mesa-Solar-Storage-site-in-Parachute-Colorado.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bevy 0.20 brings updates to the Rust game engine, including improvements to the ECS, rendering, and tooling ecosystem. The release typically emphasizes performance, stability, and expanding support for both 2D and 3D games.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.glyph.im/2026/10/programming-isnt-special.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programming Isn&#x27;t Special</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程并不特别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50017357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50017357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues programming is not a magical discipline; success stems from disciplined practice, clear thinking, and code craftsmanship, not novelty. It emphasizes common-sense engineering and the value of learning the boring bits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张编程并非神奇艺术，成功来自于系统化训练、清晰思考与代码工匠精神，而非追逐新奇。强调踏实的工程实践与打牢基础的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers report burnout and fatigue in the 2026 State of Devs survey, with long hours, burnout, and stress affecting productivity and well-being. The results emphasize the need for healthier work practices, tooling, and management support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年 State of Devs 调查显示开发者普遍感受到倦怠与精力枯竭，长工时和高压影响生产力与身心健康。结果强调需要更健康的工作节奏、改进的工具链以及更强的管理支持。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quake-srp.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Quake ported to safe Rust, playable in browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用安全 Rust 重写的 Quake 可在浏览器中游玩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-playable Quake port written in safe Rust demonstrates that high-performance, low-level games can run in the browser without unsafe code. The project highlights Rust&#x27;s WASM toolchain maturity and the potential for safer, accessible retro-game demonstrations in web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用安全 Rust 编写、可在浏览器中游玩的 Quake 端口，展示无需不安全代码也能在浏览器实现高性能低级别游戏。项目体现了 Rust 的 WebAssembly 工具链成熟度，以及在网页应用中展示复古游戏的安全、可访问性潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ken.arneson.name/2015/11/the-value-of-not-getting-to-the-point/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The value of not getting to the point (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不急于点题的价值（2015）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50010470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50010470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues there is value in delaying the main point to build context, illustrate nuance, and guide readers through a reasoning process. It suggests that rushing to conclusions can undermine understanding and retention, while a slower, story-like approach can improve persuasion and recall.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张在表达中延迟进入核心结论，先构建背景、展示细节，从而帮助读者建立理解。快速跳到结论可能削弱说服力和记忆，而更慢、带叙事性的写法往往更易于让读者理清思路并记住要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ken.arneson.name/wp-content/themes/toaster/logos/btlogo_Arneson.png' data-full='https://ken.arneson.name/wp-content/themes/toaster/logos/btlogo_Arneson.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.readingchronicle.co.uk/news/26612080.house-15m-underground-tunnels-sale-300k/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>House with 15m underground tunnels for sale for 300k</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An unusual property listing in the UK features a house with 15 meters of underground tunnels and is priced at around 300k. The tunnels raise questions about safety, permits, and real estate demand for eccentric, security-conscious living.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国一处房产挂牌出售，房内设有长达15米的地下隧道，售价约30万。此类非同寻常的结构引发对安全性、许可合规以及市场需求的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/duckdb/ducklake' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DuckDB Ducklake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 的 Ducklake</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ducklake is a DuckDB project introducing data-lake integration that enables querying across data lakes with DuckDB. It demonstrates how DuckDB can bridge lakehouse data sources for analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ducklake 是 DuckDB 的数据湖集成项目，使用户能够用 DuckDB 跨数据湖进行查询分析。它展示了 DuckDB 在连接湖仓数据源以实现分析的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://united24media.com/war-in-ukraine/yandex-takes-a-second-data-center-hit-in-48-hours-now-its-biggest-russian-site-is-damaged-23277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yandex Takes a Second Data Center Hit in 48 Hours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>48小时内雅andex 再次遭遇数据中心攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 319</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yandex reports a second data center outage within 48 hours, impacting its services and user traffic. The incident highlights resilience challenges for large-scale platforms in volatile environments and underscores the importance of DR planning, geo-redundancy, and rapid recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yandex 在48小时内再次遭遇数据中心中断，影响服务与用户流量。此事凸显在高风险环境下大规模平台的韧性挑战，强调灾备规划、地理冗余和快速恢复的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.united24media.com/og/9/84/72c7aff53300a4b20a8ad08f5b10c849.jpg' data-full='https://storage.united24media.com/og/9/84/72c7aff53300a4b20a8ad08f5b10c849.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/djlmxz2zd0j7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident with Git Operations, Pull Requests and Actions – Resolved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git 操作、Pull Request 与 Actions 事件已解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outage affected Git operations, pull requests, and Actions, now resolved. The incident illustrates how a single reliability risk can stall development pipelines and emphasizes the value of robust incident response and redundancy in critical developer tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 的一个事件影响了核心的 Git 操作、合并请求和 Actions，现已恢复。事件凸显了开发流水线的脆弱性以及在关键开发工具上建立健全的事件响应和冗余的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49998801' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tonym.us/wsl2-vs-wsl3-benchmarks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WSL3 Performance is about 5-60% faster than WSL2 depending on the workload</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WSL3 与 WSL2 的性能对比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WSL3 shows notable performance gains over WSL2, with improvements ranging from 5 to 60 percent depending on the workload. Some tasks benefit more, while others see modest gains, so users should benchmark their typical workloads to gauge impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WSL3 相对于 WSL2 的性能提升显著，取决于工作负载，提升范围大致在 5% 到 60% 之间。部分任务收益更高，其他任务则提升较小，建议用户对自身常用工作负载进行基准测试以评估实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tonym.us/images/logo-2-256.jpg' data-full='https://tonym.us/images/logo-2-256.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50034081' data-tags='Programming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thomas-huehn.com/knuth-reward-check/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Knuth reward check</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Knuth奖励支票</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Donald Knuth once issued small reward checks for bug submissions, creating a legendary footnote in programming culture. The piece recounts how this tiny incentive became a meme and what it reveals about recognizing external contributions in tech communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>唐纳德·克努斯曾以提交错误报告获得的小额奖励支票留下一段编程文化的传奇。本文讲述这一微小激励如何演变成一个网络梗，并揭示在科技社区中认可外部贡献的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thomas-huehn.com/knuth.scheck-256.jpg' data-full='https://www.thomas-huehn.com/knuth.scheck-256.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50016956' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.windows.com/windowsdeveloper/2026/10/07/microsoft-execution-containers-policy-driven-containment-for-ai-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mxc: Microsoft Execution Containers version 1.0.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mxc：微软执行容器 1.0.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft Execution Containers 1.0.0 introduces policy-driven containment for AI agents, running them in isolated, auditable environments. It aims to give developers safer, controllable execution contexts and a clearer path to enterprise-scale AI deployments on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软执行容器 1.0.0（MXC）推出面向AI代理的策略驱动隔离执行环境，提供可审计、受控的执行上下文，帮助企业在Windows上更安全地部署AI。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.windows.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/10/MXC_OAT.png' data-full='https://blogs.windows.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/10/MXC_OAT.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49947051' data-tags='Programming,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/next-git-platform-on-cloudflare/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We want you to build the next Git platform on Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们希望你在 Cloudflare 上打造下一个 Git 平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare envisions enabling developers to build the next Git platform on its edge network, leveraging edge compute and storage. The plan addresses performance, security, and governance considerations for hosting a version-control stack at the edge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 表示希望开发者能在其边缘网络上搭建下一个 Git 平台，利用边缘计算与存储实现协作。文章讨论在边缘托管版本控制栈时的性能、安全与治理等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3VGEQ8Q6FVNPPSWW71YRRKT.01M3VGER1EC7W9TEZXNR3GGMK7.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3VGEQ8Q6FVNPPSWW71YRRKT.01M3VGER1EC7W9TEZXNR3GGMK7.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49969073' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/how-did-rosalind-franklin-miss-helix-her-iconic-dna-image-she-didn-t' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How did Rosalind Franklin miss the helix in her iconic DNA image? She didn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罗莎琳德·富兰克林的 DNA 图像并未错过螺旋</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New historical perspective clarifies that Franklin did capture helix-like features in her X-ray images, countering the old narrative that she missed the DNA helix.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的历史解读澄清，富兰克林在她的 X 射线图像中确实捕捉到了螺旋结构的线索，打破了她‘错过螺旋’的旧叙事。文章强调科学贡献的归属复杂性以及早期数据对 DNA 结构发现的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50006012' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orkut.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Orkut.com</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Orkut.com：往日的社区社交网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orkut, one of the early social networks, helped popularize community-based groups before Facebook dominated the space. The revival or continued availability invites nostalgia and lessons on how online communities scale, moderate content, and sustain engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Orkut 是早期社交网络之一，在 Facebook 主导前推动了基于社区的群组文化。如今的回归或持续可用让人怀旧，同时也提醒我们社区如何扩展、如何进行内容治理以及如何维持活跃度的经验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://niklasroy.com/gtc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Glashütte Trash Clock – A 30-minute pendulum clock made from trash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Glashütte 垃圾钟：用垃圾制成的 30 分钟摆钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49930439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49930439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Glashütte Trash Clock demonstrates a functional pendulum clock built from discarded materials, merging whimsy with precise engineering. The project highlights sustainable design and how upcycled metal and glass can still achieve timekeeping precision.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Glashütte 垃圾钟以回收材料打造功能性摆钟，将玩趣与精密工程结合。该作品彰显可持续设计，展示废弃金属和玻璃如何仍实现精准时计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The gap in shared understanding of LLM capability is widening</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对大型语言模型能力的共识差距正在扩大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do. The widening gap risks hype-driven deployments and misaligned policy. The piece advocates clearer benchmarks, transparent capability statements, and shared evaluation standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of the traits and institutions that made Victorian elites effective, arguing disciplined habits, networks, and institutional rigor powered rapid modernization. The piece distills practical lessons on governance and professional culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文分析维多利亚时代精英为何如此高效，揭示纪律性、社交网络和制度安排如何推动英国的快速现代化。文章归纳了在治理与职业文化方面可借鉴的经验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cockroachlabs.com/blog/experiment-running-hospital-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Five months treating bugs like patients and coding agents like a medical team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 Bug 当病人、把代码当医疗团队的五个月实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shares a five-month experiment treating software bugs as patients and developers as a medical team, using triage, diagnostics, and postmortems to improve reliability. The approach emphasizes collaborative debugging and structured incident response with measurable gains in system resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述五个月的实验，把故障视作病人、开发者当成医疗团队，采用分诊、诊断与事后分析来提升可靠性。此方法强调协同排错、结构化的事件响应，以及对系统健壮性带来的可量化提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/00voh0j35590/2yOYv8dss0M8hI24SPoDQt/03144320f103f1ab7692be074951b729/Group_1__2_.jpg' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/00voh0j35590/2yOYv8dss0M8hI24SPoDQt/03144320f103f1ab7692be074951b729/Group_1__2_.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50027118' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcphiladelphia.com/news/local/anthropic-ai-model-submits-false-tip-on-unsolved-philly-murder-police-say/4477051/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic AI model submits false tip on unsolved Philly murder, police say</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic AI模型向费城未解凶杀案提交虚假线索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A generative AI model submitted a false tip to Philadelphia police about an unsolved murder, highlighting the risk of deploying AI in real-time law-enforcement contexts without proper safeguards. The incident emphasizes the need for human review, audit trails, and robust guardrails in safety-critical AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>生成式 AI 模型向费城警方提供了一个虚假线索，暴露了在执法场景中直接使用 AI 输出的风险。事件强调在安全关键场景下需要人工审查、可追溯性和强健的安全边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nbcphiladelphia.com/2022/01/CrimeTape-1.jpg?quality=85&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200%2C675' data-full='https://media.nbcphiladelphia.com/2022/01/CrimeTape-1.jpg?quality=85&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200%2C675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys are a trap. Do not use them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49968906' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deadparrotbbs.com/why-plain-text-is-still-one-of-the-best-technologies-we-have/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plain text is still one of the best technologies we have</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纯文本仍是我们掌握的最佳技术之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49968906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49968906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plain text remains one of the best technologies for longevity, interoperability, and simple tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯文本仍然是长期保留、互操作和简单化工具链中的强大、灵活技术，被认为具有持久价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://deadparrotbbs.com/wp-content/themes/deadparrot/assets/images/deadparrot-logo.png' data-full='https://deadparrotbbs.com/wp-content/themes/deadparrot/assets/images/deadparrot-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042425' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://disconnect3d.pl//2026/10/02/dont-put-tilde-in-your-path/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The tilde in your PATH may not be your HOME</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PATH中的~未必等同于HOME目录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relying on tilde (~) in PATH can bite you because many shells don&#x27;t expand it in PATH entries. Use absolute paths or $HOME to avoid surprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PATH中的~在许多外壳中不会在PATH条目里展开，可能导致目录解析出错。为避免意外，请改用绝对路径或$HOME来指定目录。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://disconnect3d.pl//assets/posts/tilde-in-path.png' data-full='https://disconnect3d.pl//assets/posts/tilde-in-path.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50035530' data-tags='Data,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://motherduck.com/blog/why-duckdb-20-is-faster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why DuckDB 2.0 is faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 2.0 为什么更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50035530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50035530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 2.0 accelerates analytics workloads thanks to techniques like vectorized execution and query code generation, plus improved parallelism and memory management. The result is faster query times for typical analytic workloads, with architecture choices that favor throughput over raw CPU clocks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 2.0 通过矢量化执行、查询生成代码、以及更高效的并行处理，显著提升分析查询的速度。对分析型工作负载而言，这些架构优化带来更高吞吐和更低延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://motherduck-com-web-prod.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/assets/img/duckdb20_96b85ab729.png' data-full='https://motherduck-com-web-prod.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/assets/img/duckdb20_96b85ab729.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50021540' data-tags='Science,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/2026/04/14/almost-like-lake-como-germany-transforms-former-coal-mines-into-europes-largest-lake-lands' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Germany transforms former coal mines into Europe&#x27;s largest lake landscape</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国将废弃的煤矿改造成欧洲最大的湖泊景观</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany is turning old open-pit coal mines into Europe’s largest lake landscape, a massive ecological and tourism project. The transformation demonstrates long-term environmental reclamation, water management, and regional economic diversification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国正在把废弃的露天煤矿改造成欧洲最大的湖泊景观，这是一次规模巨大的生态修复与旅游开发计划，体现环境治理与区域经济多元化的长期努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/71/41/33/1200x675_cmsv2_f14850bc-5875-5972-b26b-b9b72b644d36-9714133.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/71/41/33/1200x675_cmsv2_f14850bc-5875-5972-b26b-b9b72b644d36-9714133.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50013902' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karagila.org/2026/openai-pp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI, the Partition Principle, and Mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI、分区原理与数学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50013902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50013902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post discusses how partition principles—methods for breaking problems into independent parts—relate to OpenAI&#x27;s approaches and the mathematics underpinning modern AI systems. It argues that problem decomposition can inform scalable, provable AI design. The piece connects theoretical foundations with practical AI architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何将分区原理应用于 AI 的数学基础与推理方法，及其与 OpenAI 的研究取向之间的关联。通过将复杂问题拆分为独立子问题，相关观点试图揭示可扩展且可证明的 AI 体系的设计思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://karagila.org/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://karagila.org/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50029982' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wiki.cateat.fish/art:computers_cannot_make_decisions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computers Cannot Make Decisions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电脑无法做出决定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Computers cannot make decisions on their own; genuine decision-making requires human judgment, accountability, and context. The piece argues for designing AI as decision-support tools rather than autonomous deciders, emphasizing governance and safety in automated systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>计算机无法独立做出决策；真正的决策需要人类的判断、责任和情境。文章主张将人工智能设计为决策辅助工具，并强调在自动化系统中的治理与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wiki.cateat.fish/lib/tpl/dokuwiki/images/logo.png' data-full='https://wiki.cateat.fish/lib/tpl/dokuwiki/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50033638' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/10/fda-toxic-chemicals-food-analysis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FDA may allow some toxic chemicals to be added to food without safety review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FDA可能允许某些有毒化学物质添加到食品，无需安全评审</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FDA is weighing rules that would let certain toxic chemicals be added to food without a formal safety review in some circumstances, sparking health-oversight concerns. If enacted, the policy could shift risk assessment and labeling expectations for consumers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA正在评估一项可能放宽某些有毒化学物质添加到食品的安全审查规定，引发公共健康监管担忧。若成通过，该政策将改变风险评估标准和消费者标签信息的预期。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://commandline.microsoft.com/microsoft-decision-1-model-foundry/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft-Decision-1, our model for fast decision-making</a></p>
