<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/10/09/what-mathematicians-should-know-about-the-lean-theorem-proverquestions-of-reliability-and-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What mathematicians should know about the Lean Theorem Prover: reliability &amp; AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lean 定理证明器：可靠性与 AI 的关系</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lean&#x27;s theorem prover raises questions about reliability and AI-assisted proof, urging mathematicians to understand its limits and strengths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 定理证明器引发了关于可靠性与 AI 辅助证明的问题，敦促数学家理解其局限与优势。文中讨论了 AI 在帮助发现证明时如何保持严格性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50000985' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/mxc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MXC - a sandboxed code execution system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MXC：一个沙箱化的代码执行系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud. It supports isolating processes, controlling resources, and auditing behavior, enabling safer experimentation and evaluation of untrusted code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。它通过进程隔离、资源限制和行为审计等机制，便于在云端进行安全的代码评估与试验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6917cd2ff73d7dae54851366035a9d3a3b0625f2bf774776f8495137d57b2a4c/microsoft/mxc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6917cd2ff73d7dae54851366035a9d3a3b0625f2bf774776f8495137d57b2a4c/microsoft/mxc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the idiom push ifs up and fors down, examining its algebraic properties, practical usefulness, and limits. The piece digs into when pushing conditionals up or folding them down helps or hurts readability and compilers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨“Push Ifs Up and Fors Down”这一习语的代数性质、实际用途及边界。分析在何时向上推进条件、向下折叠有助于可读性和编译器优化，何时会带来代价。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/M-Abozaid/esp32-c3-adblock' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ESP32-C3 Adblock</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。该方案展示了消费级物联网设备如何通过过滤广告与追踪来提升隐私与性能。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective explains why unikernels never took off at scale, detailing the historical hurdles and how the landscape shifted toward containers and WebAssembly. The piece reflects on lessons learned for niche, security-focused compute.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/technology/2026/10/09/chatgpt-users-iran-planted-ai-generated-articles-us-news-media/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iranian campaign planted fake articles in real U.S. publications using ChatGPT</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗行动利用 ChatGPT 在美国主流出版物投放伪文章</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iranian campaigns used ChatGPT to generate fake articles and seeded them into real U.S. publications, attempting to sway public opinion. The incident demonstrates AI-generated misinformation’s risk to media integrity and the need for stronger verification, attribution, and detection in publishing platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗行动利用 ChatGPT 生成伪文章并投放至美国主流出版物，试图影响公众舆论。此事凸显 AI 生成的错误信息对媒体公信力的风险，促使出版平台加强核验、溯源与检测能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vienna and Beijing teams built the first thorium-229 nuclear clocks, a milestone for precision timekeeping and fundamental physics. Nuclear clocks could surpass traditional atomic clocks in stability, but practical deployment remains years away, with implications for metrology and physics experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维也纳与北京团队研发出全球首个钍-229 核钟，是在精密计时和基础物理研究方面的里程碑。核钟有望在稳定性上超越传统原子钟，但落地仍需时日，影响计量学与物理实验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-use-autoregressive-diffusion-to-generate-market-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you use autoregressive diffusion to generate market data?</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores using autoregressive diffusion models to generate synthetic market data for backtesting and research. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评估使用自回归扩散模型生成市场数据以用于回测和研究的可行性，讨论在保留真实统计性质与避免数据泄露之间的权衡，以及需面对的市场动态匹配挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI applied to centuries of archives surfaces discoveries like a forgotten meteorite and extinct rhinos, illustrating AI&#x27;s potential to aid historians and conservation. It demonstrates how search, OCR, and reasoning can unlock overlooked material.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 AI 应用于几十到上百年的档案检索，揭示了被遗忘的陨石和消失的犀牛等线索，显示 AI 在史料研究和保护工作中的潜力。该案例展示了文本检索、光学字符识别与推理等技术如何挖掘被长期忽视的材料。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.broadinstitute.org/news/clinical-trial-prion-disease-drug-candidate-begins-enrolling-participants' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clinical trial of a prion disease drug candidate begins enrolling participants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>朊病毒病药物候选物进入临床试验并开启招募</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinical trial for a prion-disease drug candidate has begun enrolling participants. Prion diseases are rapidly fatal and historically lack approved therapies, so the trial represents a milestone for therapeutic development and neurodegenerative research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>朊病毒病药物候选物已开启临床试验招募，朊病毒病强调治疗空缺，此举被视为神经退行性疾病治疗研究的重要进展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-10-chernobyl-particles-reveal-unexpectedly-stable.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chernobyl particles reveal unexpectedly stable nuclear fuel after 40 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>切尔诺贝利粒子揭示40年后核燃料的稳定性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers find that certain nuclear fuel particles from the Chernobyl fallout remain unexpectedly stable after four decades under intense radiation. The finding informs long-term waste management, containment strategies, and material performance under extreme conditions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现，切尔诺贝利碎片中的某些核燃料粒子在强辐射下经过40年仍保持出人意料的稳定性。这一发现为长期废物管理、密封与材料在极端条件下的性能提供新见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/the-lingering-legacy-o.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/the-lingering-legacy-o.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitchellh.com/writing/program-status-osc7501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Terminal Protocol for Program Status (OSC 7501)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于程序状态的终端协议（OSC 7501）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a terminal-based protocol to expose program status, enabling lightweight status reporting in CLI apps. OSC 7501 seeks to standardize health and progress signals for tooling and orchestration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种用于在终端暴露程序状态的协议，使命令行应用可以轻量级地报告健康与进度。OSC 7501 旨在标准化状态信号，便于工具链和作业编排使用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pewresearch.org/global/2026/10/01/across-the-globe-people-increasingly-say-social-media-is-harming-democracy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Across the Globe, People Increasingly Say Social Media Is Harming Democracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球范围内越来越多人认为社交媒体正在损害民主</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A global Pew study reports rising concern that social media harms democracy, with respondents linking platforms to polarization, misinformation, and manipulation. The findings highlight the need for policy, platform design changes, and media literacy to shore up democratic processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球民调显示越来越多人认为社交媒体在损害民主，指向平台引发的两极化、错误信息与操纵等问题。结果强调在政策、平台设计和媒体素养等方面需要干预，以维护民主过程。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide for Rust developers on C interop and FFI, covering how to call into C from Rust, share data safely, and common pitfalls when mixing languages. It helps Rust users decide when and how to leverage C for performance or ecosystem reasons.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/01/worlds-first-enhanced-geothermal-power-plant-completed-in-just-23-months/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First enhanced geothermal power plant completed in just 23 months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球首座增强型地热电站仅用23个月完工</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The world&#x27;s first enhanced geothermal power plant was completed in 23 months, setting a record pace for rapid deployment of Enhanced Geothermal Systems (EGS). The milestone demonstrates the potential to scale baseload clean energy with faster deployments and reduced risk, signaling a path toward broader geothermal adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球首座增强型地热电站用时仅23个月完工，刷新相关项目的施工速度记录。该成就证明通过增强地热系统可以实现更快部署与风险降低，从而扩大稳定清洁能源的潜力，推动地热在全球的更广泛应用。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizenlab.ca/toronto-based-vpn-provider-plans-to-quit-canada-over-lawful-access-bill/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Toronto-Based VPN Provider Plans to Quit Canada over Lawful-Access Bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多伦多 VPN 服务商计划因法定访问法案退出加拿大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Toronto-based VPN provider plans to quit Canada over a lawful-access bill, citing compliance burdens and potential data-access requirements. The move underscores the friction between user privacy protections and government surveillance laws, with implications for Canadian users and the VPN market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家多伦多 VPN 提供商计划因法定访问法案退出加拿大，指称合规负担与潜在数据访问要求。此举凸显用户隐私保护与政府监控法规之间的冲突，对加拿大用户与 VPN 市场产生影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/10/08/what-should-we-tell-our-students/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What should we tell our students?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们应该对学生说些什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50015236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50015236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terry Tao reflects on the messages educators should convey to students, balancing curiosity, problem‑solving mindset, and realistic expectations. The note argues for cultivating deep understanding and resilience over chasing quick results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩在博文中讨论教师应向学生传达的核心信息，强调培养好奇心、解决问题的思维方式以及务实的期望。主张重视扎实理解与韧性，而非追求短期成就。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openscad.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenSCAD the Programmers Solid 3D CAD Modeller</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenSCAD：程序员的3D CAD建模工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSCAD is a script-based 3D CAD modeller favored by developers for parametric, reproducible designs. It emphasizes code over interactive GUI, enabling precise, repeatable models and easy versioning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSCAD 是一款以脚本驱动的3D CAD建模工具，面向开发者的参数化、可重复设计。它强调以代码取代繁琐的图形界面，便于生成可重复、可版本化的模型。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A macOS project demonstrates on-device AI indexing for every photo and video frame, enabling fast, semantic search without cloud data. The approach emphasizes privacy and offline capabilities, showing potential for smarter photo management on desktops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 macOS 项目展示了在本地对每张照片、每一帧视频进行 AI 索引，以实现快速的语义搜索。该方法强调隐私与离线能力，给桌面端的照片管理带来更智能的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Classic PC demoscene productions are being ported to run natively in the browser, preserving visuals and audio with modern tech. The project showcases how WebAssembly and related tools bridge retro demos and contemporary web platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经典 PC demoscene 作品正原生在浏览器中运行的项目，努力保留原始画面与音效，借助现代技术实现。该工作展示了 WebAssembly 等工具如何让怀旧作品与现代网页平台相连。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielchasehooper.com/posts/typechecked-generic-c-data-structures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Type Safe Generic Data Structures in C (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 语言中的类型安全通用数据结构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece explores building type-safe generic data structures in C, likely using macros, _Generic, or tagging techniques to avoid void* pitfalls. It shows how to achieve more robust and maintainable generic abstractions in a language without built-in generics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨在 C 语言中构建类型安全的通用数据结构，可能借助宏、_Generic 或标签等技术，避免 void* 的风险。展示了在无内建泛型的语言中实现更健壮、可维护的通用抽象的方法。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49998992' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/archaeologists-are-reconstructing-the-invisible-technologies-of-the-stone-age-from-rope-to-thread-and-twine-180989534/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archaeologists Are Reconstructing the &#x27;Invisible&#x27; Technologies of the Stone Age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>考古学家正在重建石器时代的“看不见的”技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists are piecing together the hidden technologies of the Stone Age—like rope, thread, and twine—to infer how ancient people manipulated fibers for construction, hunting, and daily life. By visualizing the steps and tools involved, researchers illuminate early material science, craft specialization, and social coordination that underpinned long-term survival.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家正拼接石器时代隐藏的技术线索，如绳索、线和细绳，推断古人如何利用纤维完成建造、狩猎与日常活动。通过还原纤维制作的步骤与工具，研究者揭示早期材料科学、工艺分工与社会协作对生存的支撑作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/64adkh0lhgYznK0eFkQeYqrV8Ek=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(600x317:601x318)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/2a/43/2a43872b-3e44-4548-8a9d-d5b7190a2469/soft-archaeology-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/64adkh0lhgYznK0eFkQeYqrV8Ek=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(600x317:601x318)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/2a/43/2a43872b-3e44-4548-8a9d-d5b7190a2469/soft-archaeology-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://betterwispr.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: BetterWispr – Free, open-source dictation for Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：BetterWispr—免费、开源的 Mac 听写工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BetterWispr is a free, open-source dictation tool for macOS, offering an offline, privacy-preserving alternative to cloud-based services. It enables local-model transcription, giving users more control over data and customization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BetterWispr 是一个面向 macOS 的开源听写应用，强调隐私与离线能力，提供本地模型和自定义配置，作为云端听写的可选替代方案。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://momo5502.com/posts/2026-10-09-game-decompilation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>500B tokens later: Letting AI agents decompile a first-person shooter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>500B 代币后：AI 代理反编译 FPS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50039092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50039092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It highlights cost and practicality of such approaches while hinting at future capabilities for game understanding. Takeaway: massive token budgets enable deeper game understanding but raise efficiency questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨使用数千亿代币的 AI 代理来反编译第一人称射击游戏，展现代理对复杂游戏进行深度分析的能力。讨论此类方法的成本与可行性，并暗示未来在游戏理解方面的潜力。要点是超大代币预算带来更深的游戏分析，但也引发效率与成本方面的质疑。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://biohub.org/news/virtual-biology-initiative-expansion/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-ready biological data: $1.8B global commitment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI就绪生物数据：全球18亿美元承诺</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50011999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50011999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A global collaboration commits $1.8B to create AI-ready biological data, standardizing and sharing datasets to accelerate biotech research. It aims to speed up model training, improve reproducibility, and advance responsible AI in the life sciences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该计划投入18亿美元，致力于打造可用于AI训练的生物数据，并推动数据标准化与共享。此举旨在加速模型训练、提升可重复性，并推动生命科学领域的负责任 AI 实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10546-z' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 5.3M-year-old deep-sea whale necropolis in the Diamantina Zone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Diamantina 区域的 530 万年前深海鲸类尸葬</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe a 5.3-million-year-old deep-sea whale necropolis in the Diamantina Zone. The site sheds light on deep-sea preservation and ancient whale ecology, enriching our understanding of early marine ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在 Diamantina 区发现一个 530 万年前的深海鲸鱼尸葬群，显示深海条件对化石保存极有利。此发现为理解史前海洋生物与深海生态提供新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10546-z/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10546_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10546-z/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10546_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2026 Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine honors breakthroughs in optogenetics and neural circuit control, highlighting how light-based techniques enable precise modulation of brain activity and potential therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年诺贝尔生理学或医学奖表彰在光遗传学与神经回路控制方面的突破，强调光基技术在精确调控脑活动与潜在治疗中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49917900' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.haskell.org/foldl-and-foldr/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Differences Between `Foldl` and `Foldr`</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Foldl 与 Foldr 的区别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49917900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49917900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Foldl and Foldr are core Haskell folds with different performance characteristics. Foldr can work with infinite lists and is lazy, while Foldl (especially without strictness) can cause space leaks; choosing between them depends on data structure and evaluation strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Foldl 与 Foldr 是 Haskell 中的两种折叠操作，有不同的性能特性。Foldr 能处理无限列表且具备惰性求值，而 Foldl（若不使用严格化）可能导致空间泄漏；选择取决于数据结构和求值策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.haskell.org/images/haskell-opengraph.png' data-full='https://blog.haskell.org/images/haskell-opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matklad.github.io/2026/10/05/benchmark-milliseconds.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Benchmark in Milliseconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以毫秒为单位的基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49967427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49967427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise look at benchmarking with millisecond timing, highlighting how timing resolution shapes performance comparisons and why choosing the right unit matters for meaningful results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要讨论以毫秒计时的基准测试，强调计时粒度如何影响性能对比以及选择合适单位以获得有意义的结果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrouter.ai/stepfun/step-5-preview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Step 5 Preview, a 1M-context MoE from StepFun, shows up on OpenRouter</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StepFun&#x27;s Step 5 Preview unveils a Mixture-of-Experts model with a 1 million token context, highlighting ultra-long-context capabilities and modular routing. The release on OpenRouter expands access to cutting-edge MoE tech for researchers and developers building AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StepFun 的 Step 5 预览展示了具备1百万上下文长度的混合专家（MoE）模型，凸显超长上下文和模块化路由能力。该版本在 OpenRouter 上发布，进一步让研究者和开发者更容易使用前沿的 MoE 技术。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一对夫妻因一则关于 Norm Macdonald 的帖子，在两年内遭遇了 55 次 SWATTING（恶作剧式警察骚扰）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Milwaukee couple endured 55 swatting incidents over two years triggered by a single online post about Norm Macdonald, illustrating serious real-world risks from online harassment and doxxing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一对来自密尔沃基的夫妻因一则关于 Norm Macdonald 的帖子，在两年内遭遇了 55 次 SWATTING（恶作剧式警察骚扰）。此事凸显网络骚扰的现实危害，以及保护受害者免受此类攻击的重要性。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49978333' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/10/6/codemode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is Codemode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codemode 是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49978333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49978333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codemode introduces a way to think about code operation modes as first class concepts. The piece argues for structured patterns that let software adapt behavior across environments with predictable outcomes, improving reliability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codemode 将代码在不同运行环境中的行为视为一等公民，开发、生产、测试等模式成为设计的核心概念。通过提出结构化模式，帮助代码在不同环境下自适应、可预测，从而提升可靠性与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-10-06-codemode-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-10-06-codemode-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The future of independence is interdependence, arguing that sustainable autonomy in tech relies on collaboration, shared standards, and networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立的未来在于互相依赖，科技生态的进步需要协作、标准和共享基础设施。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeremyreimer.com/rockets-item.lsp?f=true&amp;p=272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The cost of lies: A Mineserver story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谎言的代价：Mineserver 故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary tale about deception within an OSS project ecosystem centered on Mineserver, exploring how misinformation, misrepresentations, or broken promises can derail maintainership and trust. The piece analyzes consequences for contributors, users, and project sustainability, and lessons for governance and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个关于 Mineserver 开源项目生态中欺骗与失信的警示故事，探讨虚假信息或承诺缺失如何影响维护者信任和项目可持续性。文章分析对贡献者、用户以及治理透明度的后果，并给出相应的教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeremyreimer.com/images/avatars/jr-avatar.jpg' data-full='https://www.jeremyreimer.com/images/avatars/jr-avatar.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50027853' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1055211/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compiling Rust to readable C with Eurydice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Eurydice 将 Rust 编译成可读的 C 代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eurydice demonstrates transforming Rust code into readable C, aiding debugging and cross-toolchain portability. This approach can help audit Rust projects and bridge interop gaps with C ecosystems, though it adds translation overhead and potential style differences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Eurydice 展示将 Rust 代码转换成可读的 C，便于调试和跨工具链移植。该方法有助于对 Rust 项目进行审核并促进与 C 生态的互操作，但会增加翻译开销与代码风格差异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49988230' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/10/hackers-obtain-counterfeit-tls-certificates-for-google-and-other-large-services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers obtain counterfeit TLS certificates for Google and other large services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客获取伪造的 TLS 证书：针对 Google 等大型服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers obtained counterfeit TLS certificates for major services including Google, enabling potential man-in-the-middle attacks or phishing. The incident underscores weaknesses in certificate authority ecosystems and the importance of certificate transparency, short-lived certs, and robust revocation processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客获取了针对 Google 等大型服务的伪造 TLS 证书，存在中间人攻击和钓鱼等潜在风险。事件凸显证书颁发体系的薄弱点，强调证书透明、短寿命证书以及强健撤销流程的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/broken-https-tls-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/broken-https-tls-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chronicles.popvax.com/p/popvax-goes-clinical' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PVX-001: open-source Covid-19 vaccine starts Phase 1 trial</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PVX-001：开源COVID-19疫苗进入I期临床试验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PVX-001, an open-source COVID-19 vaccine candidate, has entered Phase 1 trials, signaling continued collaborative, transparent vaccine development. If successful, the open-source model may accelerate governance, data sharing, and equitable access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源 COVID-19 疫苗候选体 PVX-001 已进入 I 期临床试验，标志着持续的协作与透明化疫苗研发。若成功，这一开放模型或将加速治理、数据共享与公平获取。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/news/ng-interactive/2026/oct/06/it-could-knock-a-whole-street-down-the-art-of-defusing-a-second-world-war-bomb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The art of defusing a second world war bomb</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二战炸弹拆除的艺术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An immersive look at bomb disposal work, detailing how experts assess, plan, and execute the safe removal of WWII ordnance. It reveals the tension, risk mitigation, and precise handwork behind each clearance, offering lessons in safety engineering and human factors under pressure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于拆弹工作的沉浸式报道，介绍专家如何评估、制定计划并安全移除二战弹药。揭示每一次清除背后的紧张气氛、风险控制与精准操作，为安全工程和高压环境中的人因管理提供启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/PowderworksCode/headstart' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emitting metadata early makes building/checking Rust up to twice as fast</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop. This approach can halve build and check times, sharpening feedback for changes and guiding compiler design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。此举对构建系统和编译器设计也有启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://movq.de/blog/postings/2026-09-30/0/POSTING-en.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>cp: -r or -R?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>cp：-r 还是 -R？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49910465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49910465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise explainer of the -r vs -R debate in cp across Unix-like systems. The post contrasts historical differences, portability considerations, and practical implications for recursively copying directories and handling symlinks. The takeaway: prefer -R for portability and POSIX-aligned behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要解释了 cp 命令中 -r 与 -R 的差异及在不同系统上的表现。文章对比了历史差异、可移植性考量以及在递归拷贝目录和处理符号链接时的实际影响。结论是为实现跨平台兼容性，宜优先使用 -R。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demo showing GameBoy emulation rendered on an e-paper display driven by an ESP32 at 30 FPS. It blends low-power displays with retro gaming hardware and demonstrates creative hardware hacks on microcontrollers. Takeaway: e-paper can handle animated content at low frame rates, offering unique energy-efficient retro gaming experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示在 ESP32 驱动的电子纸显示器上实现 GameBoy 模拟，帧率约 30fps。展示低功耗显示与复古游戏的结合，体现微控制器上的硬件创新潜力。要点是电子纸在低功耗条件下也能承载动画化的复古游戏体验。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valen introduces a novel memory-safety borrowing model that aims to combine safety with greater flexibility. It contends with shared mutable state and concurrency more gracefully, potentially influencing future language design, and edging toward Rust-like ergonomics with different guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valen 提出一种新颖的内存借用模型，试图在保持安全性的同时放宽检查约束。它可能让共享可变状态和并发代码的使用更为高效、易用，并对未来的语言设计产生影响。若落地，或模糊像 Rust 的所有权与传统垃圾回收之间的边界。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Smalltalk 的起源，梳理其对面向对象设计、交互式开发环境以及现代开发工具的深远影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49984854' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-020-73426-0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The role of cat eye narrowing movements in cat–human communication (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>猫眼眯眼动作在猫-人沟通中的作用（2020）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49984854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study examines how cats use pupil constriction as a social cue to humans, complementing vocalizations and body language. Findings suggest humans respond to these cues, revealing nuanced interspecies communication and practical welfare implications for pet care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现猫在与人互动时通过瞳孔收缩等方式传达信号，辅以叫声和身态语言。结果显示人类对这些信号有反应，揭示了物种间沟通的细微之处及对宠物福利的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41598-020-73426-0/MediaObjects/41598_2020_73426_Fig1_HTML.jpg' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41598-020-73426-0/MediaObjects/41598_2020_73426_Fig1_HTML.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details a DIY automation of a film scanning pipeline, integrating hardware and software to streamline scanning, color correction, and archival workflow. The project reduces manual toil and yields more consistent results for analog photography enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了将 35mm 胶片扫描流程自动化的自制方案，结合硬件与软件实现扫描、色彩校正和归档工作流的自动化。该项目降低人工劳动强度，提升成像一致性，适合胶片爱好者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49933476' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.redhat.com/articles/2026/10/02/benchmarking-ai-decision-models-against-traditional-guardrails' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decision models like Jev don&#x27;t beat LLM-as-a-judge or traditional classifiers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>像 Jev 这样的决策模型并不胜过 LLM 作为裁判</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Benchmarking shows decision models like Jev do not consistently outperform LLM-as-judge or traditional classifiers for guardrail-style tasks. The takeaway is that simple, well-tuned baselines can rival newer, more complex decision systems in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基准测试表明，像 Jev 这样的决策模型在守卫任务上并不始终优于将大型语言模型作为裁判或传统分类器。结论是，简单且调优良好的基线系统在实际应用中往往能与更复杂的决策模型相抗衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developers.redhat.com/sites/default/files/styles/share/public/Red-Hat-AI.png?itok=QCWQOeIo' data-full='https://developers.redhat.com/sites/default/files/styles/share/public/Red-Hat-AI.png?itok=QCWQOeIo' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tor Project clarifies its relationship with Mullvad, addressing funding, governance, and independence concerns. The note reassures users about how the collaboration impacts anonymity and trust.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50004115' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/article/3178-oled-burn-in-test/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OLED burn-in test: 30-month update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OLED烧屏测试：30个月更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004115' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004115' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 30-month update on OLED burn-in tests shows ongoing image retention risks despite improvements in newer panels. The study underscores that static content and uniform brightness patterns still pose a risk, though real-world usage mitigates some effects. For developers and manufacturers, it emphasizes the need for software features like pixel shifting and content variety to extend panel life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 OLED 烧屏的长期测试在30个月更新中显示，尽管新一代面板有所改进，但仍存在残留影像的风险。研究指出静态内容和均匀亮度模式易诱发烧屏，实际使用可以缓解部分问题。对于开发者与厂商，这强调了像素位移等软件特性和内容多样性以延长屏幕寿命的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techspot.com/articles-info/3178/images/2026-10-07-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.techspot.com/articles-info/3178/images/2026-10-07-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux users can now mount Proton Drive as a filesystem, enabling native access to encrypted cloud storage. The development expands secure-by-default cloud storage options for Linux users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 用户现在可以将 Proton Drive 挂载为文件系统，直接本地访问经过加密的云存储。此进展为 Linux 用户扩展了默认安全性高的云存储选项。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yossarian.net/til/post/rust-s-derive-often-implies-inline/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust&#x27;s derive often implies inline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 的 derive 常常意味着内联</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust derive macros often result in generated methods being inlined, influencing binary size and performance. The post argues developers should be mindful of how inlining interacts with code generation and optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出 Rust 的 derive 宏经常生成的方法会被内联，这会影响二进制大小和性能。建议开发者注意内联与自动生成代码之间的关系，并据此优化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.twz.com/air/former-sr-71-engineer-talks-nasas-blackbird-revival-program' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Former SR-71 engineer talks NASA&#x27;s Blackbird revival program</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former SR-71 engineer discusses NASA&#x27;s Blackbird revival efforts, highlighting technical challenges and feasibility considerations for operating a hypersonic, spy-plane heritage program. <div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ode to early chat apps, this piece sketches reviving iChat-like audio/video conferencing with modern UX: lightweight, secure, and easy to join. It argues that a simple, elegant conferencing tool could cut friction in remote work, countering feature bloat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文怀旧地设想以 iChat 为灵感的现代化音视频会议工具，强调简洁、易用与安全性。作者认为，去繁就简的会话工具能降低远程办公的使用门槛，抵抗功能臃肿。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gamehistory.org/5k-magazines/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VGHF Digital Archive passes 5000 magazines. Here&#x27;s what&#x27;s next</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VGHF 数字档案突破 5000 本杂志，接下来有哪些计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The VGHF Digital Archive has surpassed 5,000 magazines, broadening access to decades of gaming journalism and scholarship. The team plans to expand the collection with higher-quality scans, richer metadata, and improved search to aid researchers and fans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VGHF 数字档案已超过 5000 本杂志，扩大了对数十年游戏新闻与学术资料的访问。未来将加强扫描质量、元数据和搜索能力，以帮助研究者与爱好者更好地发现与理解资料。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openssh.org/releasenotes.html#10.6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenSSH 10.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenSSH 10.6</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSH 10.6 ships with security fixes and quality improvements, including updated defaults and deprecation of older algorithms. The release continues to strengthen the secure remote access tool used across servers and development environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSH 10.6 发布，包含安全修复和质量改进，更新默认设置并逐步淘汰旧算法。此版本持续提升广泛用于服务器与开发环境的远程安全访问工具的稳定性与兼容性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50001234' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-11111-4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Retrofitting language models to operate over bytes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让语言模型直接按字节处理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50001234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50001234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nature paper retrofits language models to operate over raw bytes instead of tokens, potentially improving multilingual robustness and robustness to text encoding. The work discusses tradeoffs in model capacity and data efficiency and hints at rethinking tokenization. Takeaway: byte-level modeling could reshape how we handle text data in ML.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nature 论文将语言模型改造为直接基于原始字节，而非分词后的标记，提升对多语言的鲁棒性与对文本编码的容忍性。文中讨论了模型容量、数据效率等权衡，并提出对分词的重新思考。要点是字节级建模可能改变文本在机器学习中的处理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-11111-4/MediaObjects/41586_2026_11111_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-11111-4/MediaObjects/41586_2026_11111_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner&#x27;s technical blog offers a historical walkthrough and a technical overview of its cloud network stack, from early components to the current architecture. It covers routing, switching, security, and performance considerations, highlighting design trade-offs and lessons learned for running scalable cloud networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner 的技术博客对其云网络栈进行了从早期组件到当前架构的历史回顾和技术概览。文章聚焦路由、交换、安全与性能等要点，揭示了在构建可扩展云网络时的取舍与经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《盲视》——沃茨的科幻长篇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49955297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49955297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blindsight is a hard sci-fi novel that pushes hard on questions of consciousness and intelligence through a first-contact scenario with an alien entity. It explores perception, cognition, and the ethics of technology in a future where biology, computation, and interface converge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《盲视》是一部硬科幻小说，通过与外星生命体的首次接触，深挖意识、认知与智能的边界。作品探讨感知、认知与技术伦理，以及生物、计算与接口在未来的交织。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49985524' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/math/tree/main/preprints/Integer-multiplication-below-n-log-n-September-23-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Integer multiplication below n log n</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>低于 n log n 的整数乘法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A theoretical preprint claims a method for integer multiplication that beats the classic n log n bound, hinting at new algorithmic ideas in number theory. If validated, it could influence cryptography and numeric algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>理论预印本提出在某些情形下的整数乘法复杂度可低于经典的 n log n 上界，揭示了新的算法思路。若被验证，其对密码学与数值算法或有影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/298be0ada1fe506d9ecd3e59041a367c32d5789671fa8f6c7636c8cbc4bba4c2/openai/math' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/298be0ada1fe506d9ecd3e59041a367c32d5789671fa8f6c7636c8cbc4bba4c2/openai/math' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50044715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50044715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece outlines practical paths for career professionals to build wealth beyond salary, focusing on compensation strategy, disciplined investing, and risk management. It emphasizes long-term planning, leveraging equity, and tax-efficient growth, with guidance tailored to engineers, managers, and entrepreneurs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文为高收入职业人士提供财富积累的实用路径，聚焦薪酬策略、投资与风险管理。强调长期规划、股权利用与税务优化以实现持续财富增长，对工程师、管理者和创业者等群体尤为相关。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mtlynch.io/why-are-coding-agents-so-dumb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are coding agents so dumb?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么代码代理这么蠢？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50020947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes why current coding assistants struggle with real-world coding tasks due to brittle reasoning and misinterpretation of intent. It advocates stronger evaluation, better prompts, and human-in-the-loop safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了当前代码代理在真实编程任务中的困难，原因包括推理脆弱、对意图的误解等。主张改进评估、提升提示质量，并在必要时引入人机协作。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alex0ptr/once' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Once: Cache CLI commands</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Once：缓存 CLI 命令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse. It speeds up repetitive tasks and improves reproducibility across sessions and machines. The tool targets developers and sysadmins who want smoother, repeatable workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。它可加速重复任务并提升跨会话、跨机器的可重复性，适合开发者与系统管理员构建更顺畅的工作流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/246ee73eee81e3f6b43f3e5d903751e688fb5588725ef3b4dbe9e031530859da/alex0ptr/once' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/246ee73eee81e3f6b43f3e5d903751e688fb5588725ef3b4dbe9e031530859da/alex0ptr/once' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://epoch.ai/publications/innovationeval' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recent AI models struggled to match a human algorithmic innovation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>近期 AI 模型难以赶上人类的算法创新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50027257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent AI models still struggle to surpass human originality in algorithmic innovation, suggesting current benchmarks undervalue true novelty. The study argues that evaluating innovation requires new metrics and cross-domain tests to gauge genuine creative problem solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最近的 AI 模型在算法创新的原创性方面仍难以超越人类。研究指出，现有基准难以衡量真正的创造力，需要新的评估方法来捕捉跨领域的创新能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://epoch.ai/assets/images/posts/2026/innovationeval/t-innovationeval.jpg' data-full='https://epoch.ai/assets/images/posts/2026/innovationeval/t-innovationeval.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50024242' data-tags='Privacy,AI,Web,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ndstudio.gov/posts/say-hello-to-rampart' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rampart: Browser native on-device PII radaction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rampart：浏览器原生的设备端个人数据保护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rampart proposes browser-native on-device handling to minimize exposure of PII by processing data locally. This approach reduces the need to trust remote servers for sensitive information and can be a building block for on-device AI and privacy-preserving analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rampart 提出在浏览器本地处理数据、尽量减少个人信息暴露的方案，通过就地处理来降低向服务器传输敏感数据的需要，为设备端AI和隐私保护分析提供基础。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ndstudio.gov/blog-posts/say-hello-to-rampart/featured.webp' data-full='https://ndstudio.gov/blog-posts/say-hello-to-rampart/featured.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hangul.ink/blog/hangul-day' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Happy Hangul Day! An RNN for Generating Korean Handwriting Strokes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩字日快乐！用于生成韩文笔画的 RNN</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50015833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50015833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental Show HN presenting an RNN trained to generate Korean handwriting stroke sequences, producing stylized Korean characters. It illustrates how simple sequence models can emulate handwriting styles and offers a playful glimpse into AI-assisted calligraphy. Takeaway: reusable idea for creative AI demos and stroke-based synthesis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示一个训练用来生成韩文字笔画序列的 RNN，输出具有风格的韩字笔画。该项目展示了简单序列模型在书写风格生成上的潜力，并为 AI 驱动的书法演示提供了有趣的灵感。要点是笔画序列生成的创造性潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50005608' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LittleBit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sub-1-Bit LLM Compression via Latent Factorization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过潜在因子分解实现 Sub-1-Bit 的大语言模型压缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes. The approach could unlock running capable LLMs on edge devices and specialized accelerators, expanding deployment scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。该方法有望让边缘设备和定制加速器也能部署强大的 LLM，拓展应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2b10e7577115bf8deb0aef82f9b0a6e688a60b522d544ddf6675f310979521e0/SamsungLabs/LittleBit' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2b10e7577115bf8deb0aef82f9b0a6e688a60b522d544ddf6675f310979521e0/SamsungLabs/LittleBit' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astrohelm.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: AstroHelm – Use your phone camera to aim a telescope or telephoto lens</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：AstroHelm——用手机摄像头瞄准望远镜/长焦镜头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AstroHelm lets you use a phone camera to aim a telescope or telephoto lens, likely by translating live video into steering commands. The project lowers the barrier to precise targeting, enabling easier, affordable astronomy setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AstroHelm 让手机摄像头实现瞄准望远镜或长焦镜头，通常通过将实时画面转化为瞄准指令来实现。这个项目降低了精确对准的门槛，让业余天文摄影更易上手、成本更低。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49951155' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://liquidbrain.net/blog/accountability-and-joy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accountability mechanisms can be joyful (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>問責機制亦可帶來快樂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Designers are exploring accountability mechanisms that feel constructive rather than punitive. The piece argues that clear feedback loops, inclusive governance, and humane incentives can make accountability something people buy into, not dread. When done right, accountability can improve trust and performance without eroding autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张问责机制不必只带来压力，应通过清晰的反馈、包容的治理和人性化激励，让参与者愿意承担责任而非抵触。若设计得当，问责可以提升信任和绩效，同时保护自主性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50000676' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blue-continuum.com/the-man-who-listens-to-whales' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using A.I. to decode communication of sperm whales</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 解码座头鲸的沟通</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using AI to decode sperm whale communication, researchers are analyzing patterns and context to infer potential meanings behind whale vocalizations. The work illustrates how machine learning can augment marine biology, uncovering insights into animal communication at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队利用 AI 解码座头鲸的沟通，分析模式与语境，尝试推断鲸声背后的潜在含义。该工作展示了机器学习在海洋生物学中的应用，能够大规模揭示动物沟通的规律。</p>
