<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/net4people/bbs/issues/672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xray-core concealed a certificate verification bypass vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xray-core 隐藏的证书验证漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks. The finding underscores the importance of verifying all certificate handling in proxy software and applying patches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。这一发现强调了代理软件中对证书处理的严格验证和及时打补丁的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dfd-tud/deda' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DEDA – Tracking Dots Extraction, Decoding and Anonymisation Toolkit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DEDA：跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化工具包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections. It highlights practical methods for analyzing and mitigating telemetry signals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。它展示了分析与缓解遥测信号的实际方法。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nightwatch is a Mac menu-bar utility that uses weather and astronomy data to tell you when the night will be clear. It exemplifies how lightweight tools can add practical value by leveraging public data sources.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historytoday.com/archive/review/neanderthals-among-us-peter-sahlins-review' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Neanderthals Among Us&#x27; review</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A review of Neanderthals Among Us examines how new findings reshape our understanding of human evolution, culture, and coexistence with Homo sapiens. It weighs methodological debates and what the evidence suggests about Neanderthal intelligence and social life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对《我们之中的尼安德特人》一书的评析探讨了新发现如何改变人类进化、文化与与智人共存的理解，并就研究方法分歧及尼安德特人智力与社会生活的证据进行讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究显示 Claude、ChatGPT 根据财富水平提供不同的购物价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49994746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bloomberg study finds Claude and ChatGPT offer different shopping prices based on user wealth; raises concerns about price discrimination in AI shopping assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>彭博社的研究发现 Claude 与 ChatGPT 对不同财富水平的用户呈现不同的购物价格，引发对 AI 购物助手定价歧视的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50017548' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ratfactor.com/cards/git-commit-size' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How big is a Git commit?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git 提交有多大才合适？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50017548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50017548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of typical Git commit sizes and why smaller, purpose-driven commits improve code review and history tracing. The piece offers heuristics for chunking changes and tips on when to break a feature into multiple commits. Why it matters: better commits speed up collaboration and reduce merge conflicts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论常见的 Git 提交大小以及为什么应保持较小、聚焦的提交以便于评审和追溯。给出将变更拆分成更小单元的经验法则和何时将功能拆成多次提交的建议。关键点在于更好的提交有助于协作，降低合并冲突。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49935038' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joshua.hu/magic-switch-easily-switch-magic-keyboard-trackpad-mouse-between-mac-macbook-macos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Magic Switch: Share Apple Magic keyboard/track-pad/mouse between two Macs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>魔术切换：在两台 Mac 之间共享 Apple Magic 键盘、触控板与鼠标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>One Apple Magic keyboard, trackpad, and mouse can be shared between two Macs with a simple setup, streamlining a two-computer workflow. The technique reduces clutter and keeps input devices in sync across machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过简单设置，一组 Apple Magic 键盘、触控板和鼠标即可在两台 Mac 之间共享，简化双机工作流。该方法让输入设备在两台机器之间无缝切换，减少桌面凌乱。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/10/05/ephemeral-testing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ephemeral Testing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>短暂测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49972008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49972008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ephemeral Testing advocates disposable, short-lived tests to accelerate feedback loops, discussing trade-offs and practicalities for fast iteration. The approach emphasizes fast failure and quick learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>短暂测试主张通过一次性、短时间存在的测试来加速反馈循环，讨论取舍与实操要点，强调快速迭代与快速学习。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frn.sh/go-gc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 40ms Go garbage collector pause caused by swap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 垃圾回收因 swap 引发的 40ms 暂停</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49959654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49959654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Go garbage collector pause of 40ms was triggered by OS swap, illustrating how paging can leak latency into a language runtime. The post suggests tuning memory usage and avoiding swap under latency-sensitive workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次由系统换页引发的 Go 垃圾回收暂停长达 40ms，展示了分页对运行时延迟的传染效应。文章建议优化内存使用并在对延迟敏感的工作负载中避免 swap。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://frn.sh/gogc/hz-strip-pauses-og.png' data-full='https://frn.sh/gogc/hz-strip-pauses-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The report underscores the broader debate over the environmental footprint of hyperscale infrastructure and the need for independent impact assessments and remedies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指控称谷歌为建数据中心进行非法砍伐森林，引发监管审查与环境担忧。文章强调超大规模基础设施的环境足迹，以及对独立环境评估与救济的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web-based ports of Halo, The Simpsons: Hit &amp; Run, and GTA, created with a &#x27;Vibe-coded&#x27; approach run surprisingly well in browsers, highlighting advances in browser performance, WASM, and the trade-off between fidelity and accessibility for classic games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>使用一种名为“Vibe 编码”的方法在浏览器中移植 Halo、The Simpsons: Hit &amp; Run 与 GTA，结果出人意料地流畅。文中讨论浏览器性能、WASM 与与保真度之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49983404' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ersc.io/blog/when-random-isnt-random-enough' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When random is not actually random enough</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当随机性并不真正足够随机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49983404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores gaps between theoretical randomness and real RNGs, highlighting biases in hardware and software entropy sources and showing that well-seeded generators can still fail statistical tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示理论上的随机性与实际随机源之间的差距，强调硬件与软件熵源可能存在偏见，即使经过精心种子也可能在统计测试中失效。此类问题对密码学和机器学习工作负载具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ersc.io/og/blog/when-random-isnt-random-enough.png' data-full='https://ersc.io/og/blog/when-random-isnt-random-enough.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the mathematical models behind geothermal energy, including heat transfer, reservoir dynamics, and optimization of extraction. The math informs design choices and efficiency improvements for geothermal systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍地热能源背后的数学建模，包括热传导、储层动力学与开采优化等内容，帮助设计与提升地热系统的效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/10/shinyhunters-extorted-boeing-spin-off-prior-to-arrests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ShinyHunters Extorted Boeing Spin-Off Prior to Arrests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ShinyHunters 勒索波音相关分公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ShinyHunters extorted a Boeing spin-off before their arrests, illustrating the threat actor&#x27;s focus on high-value targets and the ongoing risk to aerospace-related supply chains. The case underscores ransomware operators&#x27; evolving tactics and the need for hardened incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ShinyHunters 在多名成员被逮捕前对波音相关分公司实施勒索，暴露出高价值目标的威胁态势以及对航空供应链的风险。此案凸显勒索软件运营商的战术演变与应急响应需求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/news/using-ai-just-10-minutes-erodes-your-ability-persist-hard-things' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using AI for just 10 minutes erodes your ability to persist at hard things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅用 AI 十分钟就会削弱你坚持困难任务的能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance. The finding has implications for education, productivity, and how tasks are structured in the age of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eZ8WWZzoaR0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Homa: The end of TCP for AI clusters [video]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Homa presents a datacenter transport designed for AI clusters that aims to outperform TCP in latency and throughput. The video outlines how this approach could change inter-node communication during large-scale model training and inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Homa 提供为 AI 集群定制的数据中心传输方案，目标是在延迟和吞吐量方面优于传统 TCP。视频展示了该方案在大规模模型训练与推理中的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dryperspective.github.io/posts/complement-of-true/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The complement of true is true, except when it&#x27;s false</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>真值的补集：总为真，除非它为假</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece delves into logical truth values and complement operations, presenting a provocative angle on how negation interacts with truth. It ties into formal semantics and highlights surprising edge cases that matter for verification, type systems, and logic programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨逻辑真值及其补集运算，提出对取非与真值关系的有趣观察，揭示在形式语义、类型系统和逻辑编程中影响深远的边界情形。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/ch1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chapter 1 of Ways of Seeing by John Berger</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《看见的方式》第一章：约翰·伯格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>John Berger&#x27;s Ways of Seeing Chapter 1 challenges conventional looking by linking perception to social context and the male gaze. It introduces how captions, framing, and the act of seeing shape meaning, not merely what is depicted. The foundation laid here continues to influence critical visual culture studies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伯杰在第一章挑战了传统的“看”的观念，指出观感受社会语境和身份权力的影响。图像的含义不仅来自画作本身，还由标题、分镜和叙述框架共同塑造。该章节奠定了对视觉文化的批评基础，至今影响设计与媒体研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/images/ch1_0.jpg' data-full='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/images/ch1_0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

For developers and policymakers, the atlas helps identify high-potential regions and plan grid and investment needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球太阳能地图汇集各地区的日照资源数据，勾勒出最具可行性的区域和现实可实现的容量。对开发者与决策者而言，该工具有助于识别高潜力区域并规划电网与投资需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49998006' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s10739-026-09866-7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Despite what Watson said, Rosalind Franklin understood structure of DNA first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尽管沃森所言，罗莎琳德·富兰克林最早理解了DNA的结构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New scholarship argues that Rosalind Franklin had key insights into DNA&#x27;s helical structure earlier than Watson and Crick, based on her meticulous X-ray diffraction work. The piece underscores how historical credit in science has often overlooked women scientists and stresses the importance of accurate attribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究认为罗莎琳德·富兰克林在沃森和克里克之前就对DNA的螺旋结构有关键理解，基于她对X光衍射数据的细致分析。此文强调历史上对女性科学家的功绩常被低估，并呼吁正确署名。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1007%2Fs10739-026-09866-7/MediaObjects/10739_2026_9866_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1007%2Fs10739-026-09866-7/MediaObjects/10739_2026_9866_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dave Rupert reflects on the value of offline life and offers guidance for balancing online dependency with real-world activities. He advocates designing tools and routines that work without constant connectivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dave Rupert 反思离线生活的价值，并给出在过度在线依赖与现实生活之间取得平衡的建议，倡导设计在不需要持续连接时也能正常使用的工具与日常。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50009904' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/p10node/k10s' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: K10s – A Clickable Kubernetes TUI (Go, Bubble Tea)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：K10s——一个可点击的 Kubernetes TUI（Go、Bubble Tea）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines. By combining a familiar CLI domain with a UI that can be navigated via keyboard or mouse, it aims to reduce the cognitive load of managing clusters in the terminal. This project highlights how terminal interfaces can augment traditional DevOps tooling without sacrificing portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。它将熟悉的命令行领域与可键盘/鼠标导航的 UI 结合，旨在降低终端管理集群的认知负担。该项目展示了终端工具如何在不牺牲可移植性的前提下变得更易用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bfb9b6660a7fa4d7b432c8f32422846b8d76b0d583df01c9e9520ed4b949fd11/p10node/k10s' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bfb9b6660a7fa4d7b432c8f32422846b8d76b0d583df01c9e9520ed4b949fd11/p10node/k10s' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opper.ai/jevman-benchmark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Jevman – AI decision models play Pac-Man</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Jevman——AI 决策模型挑战 Pac-Man</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50007993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jevman uses AI decision models to play Pac-Man, serving as a benchmark for how agents plan and react in a classic arcade environment. It highlights the capabilities and limits of current AI decision systems when faced with real-time gaming challenges and simple rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jevman 将 AI 决策模型用于玩 Pac-Man，作为测试 AI 代理在实时游戏环境中的计划与反应能力的基准。该示例揭示了现阶段 AI 决策系统在面对简单规则和即时挑战时的潜力与局限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anderegg.ca/2026/10/08/i-guess-im-using-webp-now' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I guess I&#x27;m using WebP now?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tests WebP&#x27;s real-world impact, weighing compression gains against quality and compatibility. It argues that WebP can reduce image sizes significantly for web apps, but adoption hinges on tooling and browser support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试使用 WebP，并讨论在压缩效率、画质和跨浏览器兼容性方面的权衡。结论是 WebP 能显著减小图片体积，但普及取决于工具链与浏览器支持。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.bawolf.com/p/you-might-want-to-try-being-less' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You might want to try being less creative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>也许你应该少一点创意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that forcing creativity can hinder practical outcomes; embracing constraints and disciplined approaches can yield clearer, more reliable results in software and product work. It challenges the notion that more imagination always equals better software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为过分追求创意可能损害实际落地的效果；在约束条件下运用更克制、系统化的方法，往往能产出更清晰、可靠的软件与产品。它质疑“越创越好”的观念，并强调务实的开发节奏。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/114111-utah-become-first-state-ai-examine-patients-prescribe.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Utah to let AI examine patients and prescribe medication without human oversight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>犹他州允许在无人工监督下让 AI 进行病人评估并开药</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Utah proposes letting AI examine patients and prescribe medication without human oversight, signaling regulatory experimentation with automated clinical decision-making. The move raises safety, accountability, and equity concerns for patient care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>犹他州提议在无人工监督的条件下由 AI 进行病人评估并开药，标志着在自动化临床决策方面的监管尝试。此举引发对患者安全、问责与公平性的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/10/a-cray-1-supercomputer-replica-from-30-obsolete-mac-minis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Cray-1 supercomputer replica from 30 &quot;obsolete&quot; Mac Minis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 30 台废弃 Mac mini 复刻 Cray-1 超级计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker builds a Cray-1 replica using 30 obsolete Mac Minis, turning a historic vector supercomputer into a tangible, low-cost homage. The project highlights challenges of scale, cooling, and software emulation but illustrates the charm of hardware archaeology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位发烧友用 30 台废弃的 Mac mini 复刻 Cray-1 的架构，成为一个有趣的复古项目。此举暴露出规模、散热与软件兼容等挑战，同时也凸显了硬件考古的魅力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49939637' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Heilbronn Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海尔布龙问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49939637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49939637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Heilbronn problem asks how to place N points inside a unit triangle to minimize the largest empty disk, a classic question in computational geometry. The write-up surveys known bounds and recent progress, with implications for mesh generation and numerical methods.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海尔布龙问题是关于在单位三角形内放置 N 点以最小化最大空圆半径的经典几何问题。文章回顾已知界限与最近进展，并讨论其对网格生成和数值方法的含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/og.png' data-full='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theverifiedpost.com/article/whooping-cranes-ultralight-costumed-pilots-operation-migration' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Whooping Cranes Learned to Migrate by Following Costumed Pilots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>白鹤通过身着道具服的飞行员学会迁徙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A conservation program uses ultralight aircraft piloted by people in costumed outfits to guide young whooping cranes along their migratory route, a landmark example of tech-enabled wildlife management. The approach helped revive a threatened species while sparking public imagination.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项保护计划让穿着道具服的飞行员乘超轻型飞机带领幼鹤沿迁徙路线飞行，这是技术驱动的野生动物管理的里程碑案例，也提升了公众的关注度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theverifiedpost.com/verdict-card/whooping-cranes-ultralight-costumed-pilots-operation-migration?v=2' data-full='https://theverifiedpost.com/verdict-card/whooping-cranes-ultralight-costumed-pilots-operation-migration?v=2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49981264' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kyushu-u.ac.jp/en/researches/view/414/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to head into VR without wearing a headset</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需戴头显也能进入VR</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Looks at ways to experience VR without a headset, from room-scale projection and projection-based systems to other immersive setups. It highlights trade-offs between immersion, accessibility, and cost, and what this means for future hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨无需头显即可体验 VR 的路线，包括房间级投影等投影式系统，并分析沉浸感、可访问性与成本之间的权衡，以及这些趋势对未来硬件的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.kyushu-u.ac.jp/contents_file/contents_files/loader/1/EnResearch/414/icon_img/893f86faf31941b43bd1d0549bdc8bd06cd50266/default/260916_Fukuahima_Thubmnail.jpg' data-full='https://www.kyushu-u.ac.jp/contents_file/contents_files/loader/1/EnResearch/414/icon_img/893f86faf31941b43bd1d0549bdc8bd06cd50266/default/260916_Fukuahima_Thubmnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49951641' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.stephenwolfram.com/2026/09/whats-the-future-for-pure-math-research-in-the-age-of-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s the future for pure math research in the age of AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 时代纯数学研究的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-assisted tools could transform how pure math is done, accelerating conjecture, proof verification, and exploration, while also raising questions about originality and rigor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 AI 兴起的时代，纯数学研究的未来可能被 AI 工具重塑，推动猜想产生、定理证明验证与探索的速度，同时也引发关于原创性与严谨性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/09/social-pure-math-v3.png' data-full='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/09/social-pure-math-v3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 M-VAVE FM-1 口袋合成器固件的逆向工程揭示了其 DSP 与控制固件的内部工作原理，便于爱好者了解和改造设备。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanochess.org/la_cueva_bbs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>La Cueva BBS in Mexico in 1993 (session replay)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1993年的墨西哥La Cueva BBS：会话回放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49987675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on La Cueva BBS in Mexico from 1993, paired with a session replay project that preserves dial-up era online activity. It highlights early online communities, their culture, and how session data can illuminate the evolution of online social platforms and networking practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1993年墨西哥的 La Cueva BBS，以及用于保留拨号时代在线活动的会话回放项目。通过会话数据，揭示早期在线社区的文化与交流方式，以及它们如何推动对在线社群演变和网络实践的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nanochess.org/img/lacueva_1.png' data-full='https://nanochess.org/img/lacueva_1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042370' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2024-02-scandinavia-farmers-slaughtered-hunter-population.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scandinavia&#x27;s first farmers slaughtered hunter-gatherer population, DNA suggests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNA 显示斯堪的纳维亚首批农民屠杀猎人-采集者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture. The finding informs debates on migration, culture, and conflict in prehistory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古代DNA研究显示，斯堪的纳维亚早期农民群体在向农耕社会过渡时，替代或暴力驱逐了猎人-采集者群体，为欧洲史前期人口动态的讨论提供新证据，涉及迁徙、文化与冲突。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2024/scandinavias-first-far.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2024/scandinavias-first-far.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/eu-text-provenance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our approach to EU text provenance rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们对欧盟文本来源规则的应对</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49966293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49966293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI discusses how it plans to comply with EU text provenance rules, including approaches to watermarking, attribution, and transparency to ensure responsible use and protect authorship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 介绍其应对欧盟文本来源规则的策略，涉及水印、出处标注与透明度等，以确保负责任使用并保护作者权益。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49976818' data-tags='Science,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gregorygundersen.com/blog/2026/04/22/brownian-motion/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brownian Motion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>布朗运动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Brownian motion is introduced as a fundamental stochastic process describing random particle paths, underpinning diffusion models in physics and finance. Understanding its Wiener process helps explain how randomness drives system behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布朗运动被定义为描述随机粒子轨迹的基本随机过程，是物理扩散和金融定价等领域模型的基础。通过韦纳过程理解其随机性，有助于把握系统行为的变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gregorygundersen.com/image/brownian/pollen.png' data-full='https://gregorygundersen.com/image/brownian/pollen.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/entertainment-arts/business/story/2026-10-10/nicolas-cage-says-he-wont-work-with-amazon-again-over-ai-dispute' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nicolas Cage says he won&#x27;t work with Amazon again over AI dispute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼古拉斯·凯奇：因 AI 纷争拒绝与亚马逊合作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nicolas Cage says he won&#x27;t work with Amazon again over AI likeness concerns, signaling a rising actor-studio stance on synthetic performances. The stance could influence licensing rules and how AI is used in future productions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尼科拉斯·凯奇表示因 AI 相关争议将不再与亚马逊合作，凸显名人对AI生成形象在影视中的使用及授权的态度。此举可能促使制片方明确 AI 使用规则并保护演员权益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/1997cfd/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8192x4301+0+582/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fba%2F94%2F7672f0ba49cbbe410a932a4025e2%2F1553730-nicolas-cage-and-lamorne-morris-2157.jpg' data-full='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/1997cfd/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8192x4301+0+582/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fba%2F94%2F7672f0ba49cbbe410a932a4025e2%2F1553730-nicolas-cage-and-lamorne-morris-2157.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cj62ylzpr6d3o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ASOS app users receive push notifications apparently sent by hackers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ASOS 应用用户疑似收到黑客发送的推送通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ASOS app users were targeted by push notifications that appeared to come from hackers, exposing a new vector for phishing or social-engineering scams. The incident underscores the need for robust verification and incident response in push channels and supplier integrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ASOS 应用用户收到的推送通知疑似来自黑客，暴露出推送通道的网络钓鱼/社交工程新风险。事件凸显在推送通道与外部供应商集成中加强验证与事件响应的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49963568' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tidesdb.com/articles/tidesdb-now-available-for-mysql/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new write and space optimized storage engine for MySQL is here</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 MySQL 推出新的写入效率与存储空间优化引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49963568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49963568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TidesDB unveils a storage engine for MySQL focused on write performance and space efficiency, aiming to reduce storage costs for write-heavy workloads. It signals growing experimentation with alternative engines to MySQL’s InnoDB for specialized use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TidesDB 推出面向 MySQL 的新存储引擎，强调写入性能与存储空间优化，目标降低写入密集型场景的存储成本。此举体现了对 MySQL 生态中替代存储引擎的持续探索。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that floating-point and integer arithmetic operate under different mathematical and computational regimes, with different precision, rounding, and representation semantics. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张浮点数与整数运算在数学与计算范式上遵循不同规则，具有不同的精度、舍入和表示语义。讨论了对编程语言设计、数值库与性能优化的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@timventura/akhetonics-photonic-computing-1-000x-faster-cpus-b9e91c470079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Akhetonics Photonic Computing: 1,000x Faster CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Akhetonics 光子计算：CPU 提升一千倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Akhetonics claims photonic computing could deliver up to 1,000x speedups for CPUs, driven by light-based processing. While the prospect is tantalizing for AI workloads and energy efficiency, real-world practicality faces material, fabrication, and integration hurdles before it becomes practical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称 Akhetonics 的光子计算有望让 CPU 达到高达 1000x 的速度提升，依赖光信号处理。对 AI 工作负载和能效潜在影响重大，但在材料、制造与整合方面仍存挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/moritzwilksch/diffle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Diffle, a keyboard-centric diff viewer with LSP support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Diffle：以键盘为中心的 Diff 查看器，支持 LSP</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs. It aims to speed up reviews and patch creation by minimizing mouse use and providing inline intelligence. Takeaway: keyboard-centric tools with code intelligence can accelerate code comparison workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。通过最小化鼠标使用并提供代码智能，能加速审查和补丁编写。要点是键盘优先的工具结合代码智能能提升效率。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49981508' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.datocms.com/blog/handling-less-common-scripts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noto means &quot;no tofu&quot;: fixing dotted circles in Myanmar text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Noto 意味着无豆腐块：修复缅甸文本中的点状圆圈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49981508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Noto font project aims to cover Myanmar script without rendering glitches like dotted circles, fixing rendering issues caused by diacritics and font shaping. The effort highlights how proper font support for complex scripts matters for accessibility and localization, even for languages with smaller digital footprints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 的 Noto 字体项目致力于让缅甸文等复杂脚本在数字环境中稳定显示，解决因变音符和字形连笔导致的点状圆圈等问题。对无障碍与本地化来说，良好的字体支持至关重要，即使对于使用者规模较小的语言也如此。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/205/1791276403-scripts.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://www.datocms-assets.com/205/1791276403-scripts.png?auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opentelemetry.io/blog/2026/otel-native-by-design/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OTel-Native by Design – Building Products That Export to Any Observability Stack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OTel 原生设计：打造可导出到任意观测栈的产品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Design products with OpenTelemetry in a backend-agnostic way so telemetry can be exported to any observability stack. It covers exporter architecture and the benefits of vendor-agnostic instrumentation, showing how this reduces lock-in while expanding interoperability across backends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出以 OpenTelemetry 的原生设计为核心，构建可对接任意观测后端的产品，不再绑定到单一栈。内容聚焦导出器架构与厂商无关的观测数据语义，说明这种做法如何降低锁定、提升对不同后端的互操作性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opentelemetry.io/blog/2026/otel-native-by-design/cover.png' data-full='https://opentelemetry.io/blog/2026/otel-native-by-design/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49975083' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anildash.com/2026/10/06/kill-unalive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We are going to kill &quot;unalive&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们要废除 unalive 这一说法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anildash critiques sanitized language around suicide and advocates returning to direct, compassionate communication online. The piece argues that euphemisms can obscure urgency and hinder support, while clear terminology can help communities respond more effectively to at-risk individuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anildash 批评在网络讨论中将自杀等话题用委婉措辞处理，呼吁回归直接而富有同情心的表达方式。文章认为委婉说法可能模糊紧急性，阻碍对处于风险中的群体提供有效支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.anildash.com/images/broken-monitor.jpg' data-full='https://www.anildash.com/images/broken-monitor.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/microsoft-office/creator-of-free-adobe-clones-unveils-open-source-microsoft-office-excel-powerpoint-and-word-replications-autodesk-tools-and-other-subscription-based-services-may-come-next' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open-source Microsoft Excel, PowerPoint, and Word replications</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源的微软 Excel、PowerPoint 与 Word 仿制品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source effort aims to recreate Excel, PowerPoint, and Word, offering free, open alternatives to Microsoft Office. The project explores compatibility, community governance, and what it means for subscription-based software ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目在尝试开源地还原 Excel、PowerPoint 与 Word 的核心功能，挑战现有的订阅制 Office 生态。该尝试结合兼容性与开源工具，或为新功能与授权模式带来新动向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://adolfoalvarez.cl/blog/2021-09-16-plumbers-chains-and-famous-painters-the-history-of-the-pipe-operator-in-r/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plumbers, chains, and famous painters: The history of the pipe operator in R</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>R 语言中的管道运算符的历史：管道运算符</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50018929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post traces the history of the pipe operator in R, from its early conceits to the Magrittr-inspired syntax that popularized piping for data manipulation. It underscores readability benefits and the culture of experimentation in data science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章追溯了 R 语言中管道运算符的历史，从起源到受 Magrittr 影响并重塑数据处理的语法。讨论了设计取舍、文化影响，以及在数据科学中可读性的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://adolfoalvarez.cl/blog/2021-09-16-plumbers-chains-and-famous-painters-the-history-of-the-pipe-operator-in-r/featured.jpg' data-full='http://adolfoalvarez.cl/blog/2021-09-16-plumbers-chains-and-famous-painters-the-history-of-the-pipe-operator-in-r/featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.primeintellect.ai/blog/prime-agent-rust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rewriting Prime Agent in Rust</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Porting Prime Agent to Rust aims to improve safety and performance, leveraging Rust’s memory safety and ecosystem. The rewrite can yield more predictable behavior and better tooling, though it may require re-implementing features and verifying compatibility with existing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 Prime Agent 重写为 Rust 版本旨在提升安全性与性能，利用 Rust 的内存安全特性与生态系统。重写有望带来更可预测的行为和更优秀的工具链，但也可能需要重新实现部分功能并确保与现有工作流的兼容性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Protein science is reshaping biotech across food, medicine, and materials as engineers design enzymes and therapeutic proteins at scale. The piece links breakthroughs in protein design to faster biomanufacturing and a broader biotech-enabled economy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解读蛋白质科学如何推动生物产业升级，从酶的工程到新型治疗蛋白，覆盖食品、健康与材料等领域。蛋白设计的突破与更快的生物制程一起，使生物科技成为经济支柱之一。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50028162' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cerncourier.com/how-strong-is-the-strong-interaction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How strong is the strong interaction?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>强相互作用到底有多强？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how the strong interaction&#x27;s strength runs with energy in QCD, describing phenomena like asymptotic freedom and confinement. The article ties precision measurements and lattice QCD to current constraints, noting why the exact coupling matters for new physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解析量子色动力学中强相互作用强度随能量尺度改变，涉及渐近自由、束缚和格点 QCD 的约束。核心是耦合常数的数值依赖性及其对新物理的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rgpu is a PyTorch device where tensors live on a remote GPU, enabling offloading and potential speedups for large models. It highlights tradeoffs in latency, bandwidth, and distributed computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rgpu 是一个让张量驻留在远程 GPU 的 PyTorch 设备，支持将计算分发到另一台主机以进行加速。它揭示了延迟、带宽与分布式计算的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/new-linux-tech-compresses-memory-in-ram-as-ram-for-452x-speedup-new-cram-method-offers-giant-boost-to-compressed-memory-reads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New CRAM method offers giant boost to compressed memory reads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新CRAM 方法为压缩内存读取带来巨大提速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose CRAM-based memory compression that stores data in RAM in compressed form, enabling hundreds of times faster reads due to CPU&#x27;s ability to decompress on the fly. The result could dramatically reduce memory bandwidth bottlenecks and extend RAM capacity without hardware changes, especially for data-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员提出基于 CRAM 的内存压缩，将数据以压缩形式存放在内存中，CPU 在解压后可实现数百倍的读取速度提升。此技术可能显著缓解内存带宽瓶颈，提升数据密集型 workload 的性能，同时在不更换硬件的情况下扩展 RAM 容量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/gfoMfGRQze7gtw7Ddg8FZM-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/gfoMfGRQze7gtw7Ddg8FZM-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fingerprints Are the New Cookies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recursive self-improvement through collective intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过集体智慧实现的递归自我提升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents. Iterative loops could push capabilities beyond a single-model ceiling, but governance and alignment become more complex at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。随着协同放大能力，进展可能超出单一模型的上限，但规模化时在治理和对齐方面的挑战也随之增加。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/assets/figures/ogp.png' data-full='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/assets/figures/ogp.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://articlesofinterest.substack.com/p/lace-and-labor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lace and Labor: Lessons from the actual Luddites</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lace and Labor：从真正的卢德分子那里学到的教训</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece drawing on actual Luddites to critique tech-era labor dynamics, arguing for worker-centered approaches to automation and digital transformation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章以真实的卢德分子为镜，批评当下科技时代的劳动关系，主张在自动化与数字化转型中以工人为中心。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49997831' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottaaronson.blog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mathocalypse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学的末日</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Mathocalypse argues that the coming era could reshape how mathematics is done, learned, and automated, driven by AI and new tooling. It provocatively explores existential questions about mathematical creativity and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《数学的末日》探讨在人工智能与新工具推动下，未来的数学研究、教学与自动化可能经历的剧变，并对数学创造力与可靠性提出挑战性问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvc2NvdHRhYXJvbnNvbi5ibG9nXC93cC1jb250ZW50XC91cGxvYWRzXC8yMDIxXC8xMFwvY3JvcHBlZC1KYWNrZXQuZ2lmIiwidHh0IjoiU2h0ZXRsLU9wdGltaXplZCIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxMjk1MjA1ODB9.siOtN7gHw4tefA_rZickBw4GfI6tPxGgOQ1AXr2ZoOQMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvc2NvdHRhYXJvbnNvbi5ibG9nXC93cC1jb250ZW50XC91cGxvYWRzXC8yMDIxXC8xMFwvY3JvcHBlZC1KYWNrZXQuZ2lmIiwidHh0IjoiU2h0ZXRsLU9wdGltaXplZCIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxMjk1MjA1ODB9.siOtN7gHw4tefA_rZickBw4GfI6tPxGgOQ1AXr2ZoOQMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://datacenters.google/locations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Data Centers around the world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌全球数据中心分布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing. The data-centre footprint underpins modern internet services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。文章点出规模、区域性考量及可持续承诺和监管影响，强调数据中心是现代互联网服务的基础设施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gstatic.com/marketing-cms/assets/images/c7/92/366b77ff476abc03a1b0a913e40b/wtils5-key-art-1920x1080.webp=n-w1440-h960-fcrop64=1,000022c9ffffdd37-rw' data-full='https://www.gstatic.com/marketing-cms/assets/images/c7/92/366b77ff476abc03a1b0a913e40b/wtils5-key-art-1920x1080.webp=n-w1440-h960-fcrop64=1,000022c9ffffdd37-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Celebrates the surprising effectiveness of Vex in NixOS, showing how this tool can streamline configurations and improve reproducibility. The write-up argues that robust tooling can dramatically simplify managing complex system setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>赞叹 Vex 在 NixOS 上的出色表现，展示它如何简化配置、提升可重复性。强调强工具集对管理复杂系统的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://almostimplemented.github.io/jazz-pianist-style/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Learning Jazz Pianist Style with Cross-Attention Conditioning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用跨注意力条件化学习爵士钢琴家的风格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers train a model to imitate jazz pianist style using cross-attention conditioning, enabling stylized music generation and potential for interactive improvisation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者通过跨注意力条件化，让模型模仿爵士钢琴家的演奏风格，从而实现风格化的音乐生成与潜在的互动即兴。文中讲述注意力机制在节奏、乐句和和声等方面引导细腻表达的方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/shader-effects-inc/shaders' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shaders, WebGPU Components for React, Vue, Svelte, Solid, JavaScript and Framer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49988709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curated collection of shader effects and WebGPU components compatible with major frontend frameworks. It accelerates building rich visuals by providing reusable, framework-friendly building blocks, lowering the barrier for GPU accelerated UI features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一套可与主流前端框架兼容的着色器效果与 WebGPU 组件合集，提供可复用的框架友好构件，帮助快速实现基于 GPU 的界面视觉效果，降低实现门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/canadian-youtuber-builds-flock-like-camera-to-exclusively-track-police-vehicles-officers-visit-his-home-to-express-privacy-concerns' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTuber builds &#x27;Flock-like&#x27; camera to track police vehicles, gets police visit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTuber 制作“Flock-like” 摄像头追踪警车，遭警察上门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A YouTuber crafts a Flock-like camera system to track police vehicles, drawing privacy concerns and a police visit. The case spotlights the tensions between DIY surveillance tech, public space monitoring, and civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名博主开发了类似 Flock 的相机追踪系统以追踪警车，引发隐私担忧并遭警察家访，这反映了自建监控技术与公民自由之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/health/the-mental-health-of-young-men-is-declining-experts-warn-it-could-get-worse-9.7361010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The mental health of young men is declining. Experts warn it could get worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年轻男性的心理健康正在下滑，专家警告情况可能会恶化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CBC article reports deteriorating mental health among young men, highlighting risk factors such as social isolation and economic stress, with experts warning it could worsen without targeted support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CBC 报道显示年轻男性心理健康状况恶化，强调社交孤立与经济压力等风险因素，专家警告若不提供针对性支持，情况可能进一步恶化。</p>
