<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atoll92.github.io/agent-standup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agents in a stand-up call</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理在站立会议中的应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50039630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50039630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of AI agents participating in stand-up meetings, delivering updates and aiding task planning, as a glimpse of human-agent collaboration in daily work. It discusses how agents cope with sparse context and what benefits or drawbacks arise. Takeaway: AI assistants can augment team rituals, but need governance to stay helpful and not noisy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 AI 代理在站立会议中的应用，负责汇报进展并协助任务规划，展示了日常工作中人机协作的前景。讨论代理在有限上下文下的表现及利弊。要点是 AI 助手可以提升团队例行流程，但需要适当的治理以避免产生干扰。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://projecttimestamper.org/blog/common-crawl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Timestamping a Giant Record of the Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为巨量网页记录打上时间戳</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50014397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50014397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Common Crawl is adding robust timestamping to its web archive to improve provenance, reproducibility, and long-term accessibility for researchers relying on open web data. This strengthens the credibility and traceability of large-scale crawl datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Common Crawl 正在其网页档案中增加强健的时间戳，以提升来源可追溯性、可重复性和长期可访问性，利于研究者依赖开源网络数据。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://databasearchitects.blogspot.com/2026/04/safe-optimistic-lock-coupling.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Safe Optimistic Lock Coupling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全的乐观锁耦合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Safe optimistic lock coupling presents a robust approach to concurrency, combining optimistic transactions with careful coupling to minimize contention while ensuring correctness. Practical guidance for building scalable, low-latency systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全乐观锁耦合提出一种对并发性更鲁棒的做法，将乐观事务与相互耦合结合，以降低竞争并确保正确性，为构建可扩展、低延迟的系统提供实用指导。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49986765' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(413) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sregym.com/blog/jev-driven-sre-diagnosis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jev-Driven SRE Diagnosis: What Worked and What Failed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JEV 驱动的 SRE 诊断：成效与不足</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49986765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An SRE diagnosis framework centered on &#x27;JEV&#x27; reveals what went right and what failed during incidents, with concrete, actionable lessons for incident response, observability, and postmortems. The piece emphasizes iterating on diagnosis criteria and tooling to improve reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以“JEV”为核心的 SRE 诊断方法揭示事故中哪些做得好、哪些失败，并给出可执行的故障应对、可观测性与事后分析的教训。文章强调在诊断标准与工具上持续迭代以提升可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sregym.com/blog/jev-driven-sre-diagnosis/share-image' data-full='https://www.sregym.com/blog/jev-driven-sre-diagnosis/share-image' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49980790' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/superdesigndev/treg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Treg (OpenRouter for Tools)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Treg：工具的开放路由器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49980790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Treg is an open router for tools, enabling dynamic discovery and routing of tool invocations in AI workflows. It helps organize, route, and orchestrate tool calls, reducing integration boilerplate and enabling scalable pipelines for agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Treg 是一个面向工具的开源路由器，支持在 AI 工作流中动态发现并路由工具调用。它整理、分发和编排工具调用，降低集成工作量，提升代理系统的可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b99a9919052b758a8962d215f2bb7ad6092d6d80fbec6cbe51cf2772e541208/superdesigndev/treg' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b99a9919052b758a8962d215f2bb7ad6092d6d80fbec6cbe51cf2772e541208/superdesigndev/treg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.americanscientist.org/article/whatever-became-of-holography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Whatever became of holography? (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全息术到底发展到了哪儿？（2011）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs. It explains why practical, widespread holo-imaging remains elusive despite decades of research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。尽管研究持续，实际大规模应用仍受材料、制造成本与可扩展性等瓶颈制约。文章提醒读者，成像质量、规模化和成本是关键挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50047071' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://richwhitehouse.com/index.php?postid=78' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artificial Insanity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工疯狂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the quirky, often surprising behavior of AI systems through the lens of &#x27;artificial insanity.&#x27; The piece calls out biases, hallucinations, and misalignment as systemic failure modes, arguing for humility about AI capabilities and better alignment practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨人工智能系统的怪诞与不可预测行为，提出“人工疯狂”视角来审视偏见、幻觉与目标错配等问题。强调不要高估 AI 的可靠性，呼吁更强的对齐与鲁棒性研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://richwhitehouse.com/images/rwlogox.jpg' data-full='https://richwhitehouse.com/images/rwlogox.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.warren.senate.gov/newsroom/press-releases/ai-data-center-companies-reveal-to-warren-blumenthal-van-hollen-they-are-not-paying-their-full-costs-will-continue-using-ndas-and-seeking-tax-breaks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Data Centers Not Paying Their Costs, Will Keep Using NDAs and Seek Tax Breaks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI数据中心不支付全部成本，将继续使用 NDA 并寻求税收减免</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Senators warn that AI data centers externalize costs, rely on NDAs to suppress disclosures, and press for tax incentives. The missive advocates policy reforms to ensure cost internalization and greater transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>参议员警告AI数据中心将成本外部化、通过保密协议压制信息披露，并寻求税收优惠。信函主张通过政策改革实现成本内部化并提高透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.warren.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Warren_Logo_Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.warren.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Warren_Logo_Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>值得打造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Worth Building argues for thoughtful decision-making about whether to build features in-house, emphasizing value, differentiation, and strategic fit. The piece provides criteria to avoid unnecessary bespoke development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在决定是否自研功能时要进行深思熟虑的权衡，强调价值、差异化能力和战略适配性，提出避免无谓的定制开发的标准与方法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://serverlesshorrors.com/all/cloudflare-108k/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Serverless Horrors $10,811.41</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Serverless Horrors：$10,811.41</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes a serverless billing nightmare, breaking down how small inefficiencies, cold starts, and vendor metering can push costs into the five-figure range. It underscores the fragility of pure serverless cost models and advocates for better budgeting and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章剖析了服务器端无服务器架构下的高额账单风险，展示了小故障、冷启动和计费方式如何把成本推高到数万美元的水平。强调了无服务器成本模型的脆弱性，呼吁建立更好的预算与成本监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://serverlesshorrors.com/assets/cloudflare-10.8k.jpg' data-full='https://serverlesshorrors.com/assets/cloudflare-10.8k.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A judge ruled that the Fort Hood attacker&#x27;s execution by firing squad will be livestreamed. The decision raises questions about media access to executions, the ethics of livestreaming capital punishment, and the potential impact on public discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官裁定， Fort Hood 袭击案凶手将被枪决并进行现场直播。这一决定引发关于死刑直播的伦理与政策讨论，以及媒体在极端司法程序中的准入边界。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50049302' data-tags='Politics,Finance,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/10/08/governments-are-mishandling-the-affordability-crisis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Governments are mishandling the affordability crisis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府在可负担性危机上的应对失当</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy responses to rising living costs appear misaligned, with subsidies and price controls offering short-term relief but failing to tackle root drivers like productivity, housing supply, and energy dynamics. Without structural reforms, affordability pressures risk fueling slower growth and social discontent. The piece argues for more evidence-based, growth-oriented policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对上升的生活成本的宏观政策反应显现错配，补贴和价格管控只能提供短期缓解，未能触达生产率、住房供给与能源结构等根本驱动。若不进行结构性改革，负担压力可能拖累增长并加剧社会不满。文章呼吁采用更具证据基础的、以提升生产力为目标的政策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clojure&#x27;s value proposition in a world where language models drive code generation and automation. The piece argues that its functional, immutable data structures and macro system can help manage AI-generated code and data pipelines with clarity and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在语言模型主导代码生成和自动化的时代，Clojure 的价值在于它的函数式、不可变数据结构和宏系统，可以帮助清晰、安全地管理 AI 生成的代码和数据管线。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICANN 公布 2026 年新通用顶级域名申请轮次</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49997301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICANN reveals the 2026 round applications for new generic top-level domains, signaling expansion in branding namespaces and potential competition for existing domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICANN 公布 2026 年新通用顶级域名申请轮次，标志着域名命名空间的扩张与潜在的竞争格局。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042107' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(438) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5vgjm3gk7rzo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is creating a &#x27;human premium&#x27; for art created by people</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在为人类创作的艺术带来“人类溢价”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI-generated art becomes more capable, buyers are increasingly paying a premium for works created by humans, driven by notions of authenticity, process, and provenance. The trend could redraw pricing dynamics in the art market and incentivize artists to emphasize craft and storytelling. It also raises questions for platforms hosting both AI and human-made works.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 生成艺术品的能力提升，购买者越来越愿意为人类创作的作品支付“人类溢价”，原因在于对真实性、创作过程与来源的认同。这一趋势或将重塑艺术市场的定价格局，促使艺术家更加强调工艺与讲述能力，并对同时展出 AI 与人工创作作品的平台提出新问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6fb8/live/8086eea0-c3cb-11f1-a64c-550be9e3c66b.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6fb8/live/8086eea0-c3cb-11f1-a64c-550be9e3c66b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50008685' data-tags='Career,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(439) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pentlander.com/the-deeply-impersonal-personalized-recruiter-mail/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Deeply Impersonal Personalized Recruiter Mail</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>极度无个性化的定制化招聘邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece critiques how recruitment emails can feel generic and transactional even when they claim personalization, offering examples and guidance on crafting more meaningful outreach. It emphasizes that genuine relevance and human touch beat templated, data-driven pings in attracting top talent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评招聘邮件即便标榜个性化，往往也显得机械化和事务化，给人冷漠的印象。作者提出提升邮件质量的做法，强调真诚相关性和人情味胜过模板化、数据驱动的 outreach。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.pentlander.com/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.pentlander.com/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50004542' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(440) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.flinders.edu.au/blog/2026/10/06/vanillin-provides-a-sweet-solution-for-chronic-wound-healing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vanillin provides a sweet solution for chronic wound healing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>香兰素（香草醛）为慢性创面愈合提供甜蜜解决方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50004542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found vanillin can promote wound healing by reducing inflammation or promoting cell proliferation. The findings point to a potential low-cost, widely available adjunct to existing therapies, meriting further clinical study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明香兰素在慢性创伤愈合中具有潜在作用，可能通过促进组织修复和降低炎症来加速愈合。该发现为现有治疗提供低成本、易获取的新辅助方案，值得进一步临床评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.flinders.edu.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1885924192.jpg' data-full='https://news.flinders.edu.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1885924192.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(441) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pavel-krivanek.github.io/The-Analog-Thing-Simulator/public/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Analog Computer Simulator in the Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器中的模拟计算机仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49966866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49966866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based analog computer simulator lets you wire up components and run signal-flow experiments without hardware. It highlights the enduring appeal of analog computation for education and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个在浏览器中运行的模拟计算机仿真器，允许你在无硬件的条件下搭线搭接并进行信号流实验。它体现了模拟计算在教育与探索中的持久魅力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49971678' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nivdayan.github.io/dostoevsky.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better Space-Time Trade-Offs for LSM-Tree Based Key-Value Stores [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>针对基于 LSM-树的键值存储的更优空间-时间权衡 [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This paper presents improved space-time trade-offs for log-structured merge-tree (LSM-tree) based KV stores, with implications for throughput, write amplification, and storage density. The result can influence database systems like RocksDB and LevelDB by optimizing compaction strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出改进的基于 LSM-树的键值存储在空间和时间上的权衡，对吞吐、写放大与存储密度等指标有影响，将为 RocksDB、LevelDB 等数据库的合并策略带来优化空间。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pushsquare.com/news/2026/10/ps5-jailbreaks-are-escalating-at-an-unprecedented-pace-and-sony-must-be-sweating' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PS5 Jailbreaks Are Escalating at an Unprecedented Pace</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PS5 jailbreak activity is accelerating at an unprecedented pace, signaling growing security and ecosystem challenges for Sony. The surge raises questions about console protection, unsigned code risks, and the balance between homebrew communities and official policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PS5 越狱活动呈现空前加速，给索尼及其生态系统带来更大安全挑战。此势头引发关于主机安全、未签名代码风险以及官方政策与玩家自制内容之间平衡的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub adds granular repository settings to control who can access and review pull requests. This gives maintainers finer control over collaboration and can improve security for large teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 新增更细粒度的仓库设置，用以控制谁可以访问和审阅拉取请求。此举为大团队的协作提供更精细的权限管理与安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>霍尔木兹海峡油轮袭击激增至战时高位，伊朗试图阻断石油</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50026509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50026509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz surge to wartime highs as Iran tries choking oil flows. This raises risk for shipping, insurance, and global energy markets, underscoring how geopolitical flashpoints threaten supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>霍尔木兹海峡的油轮袭击激增至接近战争水平，伊朗试图切断石油运输。此举提高了航运、保险与全球能源市场的风险，突显地缘政治冲突对全球供应链的冲击。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pocketty.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pocketty – iPhone SSH terminal that pings you when an agent is blocked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Pocketty——iPhone SSH 终端，代理被阻断时会提醒你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50009634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pocketty offers an iPhone SSH terminal with monitoring that notifies you when the SSH agent becomes blocked, helping maintain awareness on mobile development workflows. It pairs a lightweight mobile client with alerting to keep connections alive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pocketty 提供一款 iPhone SSH 终端，具备在代理被阻断时给出通知的监控功能，帮助移动端开发工作流保持连接可用性。该应用将轻量级客户端与告警机制结合，提升运维与开发效率。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/10/261005011249.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stanford scientists found a way to regrow cartilage and stop arthritis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯坦福科学家找到再生软骨并有望阻止关节炎进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford researchers regrow cartilage and potentially halt arthritis progression, likely via tissue engineering or stem cell approaches. If validated, this could transform treatment for joint diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福大学的研究团队提出一种再生软骨的方法，可能阻止关节炎进展，或通过组织工程/干细胞等途径实现。若转化为临床应用，可能彻底改变关节疾病的治疗。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The report details that Cloudflare’s 1.1.1.1 DNS service remains unblocked from piracy-related blocking and avoids penalties in France due to the regulatory regime. It highlights ongoing tensions between anti-piracy enforcement and privacy or DNS privacy advocates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出，Cloudflare 的 1.1.1.1 DNS 服务未被纳入盗版拦截，也因此在法国避免罚款。这反映了反盗版执法与隐私、域名解析政策之间的持续博弈与不确定性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(452) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://igoro.com/archive/gallery-of-processor-cache-effects/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gallery of Processor Cache Effects (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>处理器缓存效应图集（2010）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective gallery visualizes how different processor caches respond to memory access patterns, illustrating cache hits, misses, and latency across architectures. The collection helps engineers reason about memory locality and optimization strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这组回顾性画廊以直观图像展示不同处理器缓存对内存访问模式的响应，揭示缓存命中/未命中及延迟在不同架构中的表现。为工程师理解内存局部性与优化策略提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50024805' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://celld.dev/docs/engineering/deterministic-simulation-testing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deterministic Simulation Testing in Celld</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Celld 的确定性仿真测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Celld introduces deterministic simulation testing to ensure reproducible outcomes in simulations, enabling researchers to trust results across runs. The approach likely involves fixed seeds and recorded state to make experiments repeatable. Takeaway: determinism is essential for reliable AI/ML simulation studies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Celld 提出确定性仿真测试，确保仿真结果在多次运行中的可重复性，从而提升研究可信度。方法可能包括固定随机种子和记录状态以实现可重复性。要点是确定性对可靠的 AI/ML 仿真研究至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://celld.dev/docs/engineering/deterministic-simulation-testing/og.png' data-full='https://celld.dev/docs/engineering/deterministic-simulation-testing/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-02921-7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US proposes $100k charge for international students to do post-graduate work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国拟向国际学生毕业后工作征收10万美元费用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The report discusses a US proposal to charge international students up to $100k for post-graduate work eligibility. If enacted, the policy could deter foreign talent, affect universities’ diversity, and reshape funding for graduate pathways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出美国拟对国际学生毕业后工作资格征收最高10万美元的费用。若实施，该政策可能削弱对外国人才的吸引力、影响高校多样性，并改变研究生路径的资金结构。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/hackers-abuse-google-ads-bing-redirects-to-push-claude-clickfix-attacks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers abuse Google Ads, Bing redirects to push Claude ClickFix attacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客滥用 Google 广告、Bing 重定向推动 Claude ClickFix 攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers abuse paid search ads and Bing redirects to push Claude ClickFix exploits, tricking users into visiting malicious pages. The campaign underscores brand-targeted phishing risks and the need for ad ecosystem monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客滥用付费搜索广告与 Bing 重定向，将用户引导至与 Claude ClickFix 相关的攻击页面，实施钓鱼或恶意软件分发。该行为凸显品牌定向钓鱼的风险，以及加强广告生态监控的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/05/07/ClaudeChats.png' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/05/07/ClaudeChats.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://metedata.substack.com/p/ai-is-throwing-a-roadside-picnic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Is Throwing a Roadside Picnic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在掀起路边野餐的隐喻讨论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI systems produce unpredictable, messy outputs that feel like a roadside picnic, complicating tooling, governance, and expectations for real-world use. It calls for more robust toolchains and governance to manage such open-ended outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为AI系统会产生难以预测、零碎混乱的产出，像路边野餐般难以驾驭，呼吁建设更强健的工具链和治理框架来管理这类开放式输出。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50033601' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shnatsel.github.io/safe-simd-in-rust-even-on-the-inside/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Safe SIMD in Rust, even on the inside</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的安全 SIMD，即便在底层也安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety. Highlights performance wins for numeric workloads and implications for libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。强调对数值工作负载的性能收益以及对库的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50024757' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/09/hey-platforms-your-violent-content-policies-are-about-to-meet-the-pentagons-firing-squad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Platforms&#x27; Violent Content Rules Are About to Meet The Pentagon&#x27;s Firing Squad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>平台暴力内容规则将面临五角大楼的严格审查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50024757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Platforms may face tighter pressure to police violent content as the Pentagon signals stricter enforcement or collaboration. The policy dynamics could reshape moderation, content removal, and safety commitments across social media and apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五角大楼释放更严格的执法信号，平台的暴力内容治理将面临更大压力。此一政策趋势可能重塑社媒与应用的内容审核、移除以及安全承诺。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mazdastories.com/en_us/inspire/paper-cranes-into-the-fold/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Legend of the Paper Crane</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纸鹤传奇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lyrical meditation on the enduring symbol of origami cranes and how small, patient efforts accumulate into meaning. The piece reframes crafts as examples of resilience and collaboration, illustrating how simple, repeatable actions can inspire communities and spark creative thinking in tech culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于纸鹤这一长期象征的抒情反思，强调耐心与积小成大的力量。文章把手工艺看作韧性与协作的范例，展示简单、可重复的行动如何激发社区灵感并在科技文化中激发创造性思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/shape-sensing-sheet-digitally-tracks-movement-bends-twists-1008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shape-sensing sheet digitally tracks its own movement as it bends and twists</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>形状感知薄片在弯曲扭动时能数字化追踪自身运动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50032253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT researchers unveil a shape-sensing sheet that digitally tracks its own movement as it bends and twists. The development promises advances in soft robotics, wearables, and real-time morphing surfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>形状感知薄片通过嵌入式传感器实时追踪自身在弯曲、扭动中的运动，为柔性材料、可穿戴设备与自适应表面带来新可能。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linuxstans.com/11-of-23-core-open-source-projects-run-on-1-or-2-people/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>11 of 23 Core Open Source Projects Run on 1 or 2 People</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>23个核心开源项目中有11个由1~2人维护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at core open source projects shows that nearly half are sustained by very small teams. Lean maintenance tends to rely on clear maintainership, automation, and modular design to keep critical infrastructure alive. This underscores that large organizations aren’t the only path to robust OSS ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>核心开源项目中近一半由极小团队维持，凸显清晰的维护分工、自动化以及模块化设计在维持关键基础设施方面的重要性。说明大型组织并非唯一的稳健开源生态之路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linuxstans.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/11-of-23-core-open-source-projects-run-on-1-or-2-people.png' data-full='https://linuxstans.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/11-of-23-core-open-source-projects-run-on-1-or-2-people.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EmDash uses Clef to moderate its plugin registry, applying identity and access controls to curate third-party extensions. The approach improves security and trust in extensible platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EmDash 采用 Clef 对插件注册表进行身份和访问控制审核，以提升扩展生态的安全性与可信度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://robotgirlgang.com/2026/10/08/robot-is-a-social-construct-why-your-dishwasher-is-not-a-robot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Robot&quot; Is a Social Construct: Why Your Dishwasher Is Not a Robot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“机器人”是社会构造：为什么你的洗碗机不是机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that &#x27;robot&#x27; is a social construct framing how we classify machines, and that domestic devices like dishwashers lack autonomy and intent to be true robots. It invites readers to rethink labeling and consider actual capabilities versus marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为“机器人”是用来界定机器的社会建构，家用洗碗机等设备并不具备自主性与意图，因此并非真正意义上的机器人。作者呼吁读者重新思考标签，并关注实际能力与营销之间的差异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://robotgirlgang.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/rgg-site-icon-master.png' data-full='https://robotgirlgang.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/rgg-site-icon-master.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49989684' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.criteo.com/blog/human-skills-ai-cant-develop-junior-engineers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Forever Junior: The Skills AI Can&#x27;t Develop for You</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永远的初级：AI 无法替代的技能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49989684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49989684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI may automate routine tasks, but junior engineers still need mentorship to build core skills like critical thinking, domain intuition, and collaboration. The piece argues that some skills remain uniquely human and hard for AI to replicate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 可以处理常规任务，但初级工程师仍需通过指导培养关键技能，如批判性思维、领域直觉及协作能力。这些能力是 AI 难以替代的核心部分。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50031289' data-tags='Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joelsiks.com/posts/aarch64-exclusive-access-cacheability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Ldaxr Doesn&#x27;t Work: Exclusive Accesses and Cacheability on AArch64</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ldaxr 不工作时：AArch64 上的独占访问与缓存性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes scenarios where Load-Exclusive/Store-Exclusive semantics break on AArch64 due to cache behavior and memory ordering. It explains how exclusive accesses interact with caches and why some synchronizations fail on certain hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文分析了在 AArch64 上由于缓存行为与内存排序导致的 Ldaxr/Stlxr 独占访问失效情形，解释了独占访问与缓存之间的交互及为何某些同步在特定硬件上会失败。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>理论计算机科学家提出对3SUM实现子平方时间、对APSP实现子三次时间的算法，推动这两类基础问题时间界限的突破。相关结果可能影响数据库、图算法以及复杂度理论的研究与应用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proves that the irrationality exponent of logarithms of rational numbers is 2, contributing to the field of Diophantine approximation and number theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文证明有理数对数的无理性指数为 2，为丢番图逼近中的一个重要结果，推动数论研究。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://restofworld.org/2026/ai-data-center-memory-chip-shortage-cheap-smartphones-digital-divide/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI boom is making the cheapest smartphones disappear</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 热潮正在让最便宜的智能手机消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49974238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49974238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI boom and memory-chip shortages at data centers are driving up costs and reducing the availability of ultra-cheap smartphones, widening the digital divide in emerging markets. The piece links hardware constraints to consumer access and growth trajectories.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能热潮及数据中心内存芯片短缺推高成本，导致极便宜的智能手机供应受限，进一步拉大新兴市场的数字鸿沟。文章将硬件约束与你的可及性和移动增长前景联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rews.cc/a/anthropic-bans-cruelty-to-claude-still-won-t-say-what-it-pro-bd99f6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Bans Cruelty to Claude, Still Won&#x27;t Say What It Protects</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 禁止对 Claude 的残酷对待，但仍不透露保护对象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50038383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50038383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic updated its safety policy to ban cruelty toward Claude and to curb abusive prompts. It stops short of clarifying exactly what content or interactions are protected. The change highlights the broader tension between explicit guardrails and transparency in AI safety decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 更新对 Claude 的安全策略，禁止对模型施以残酷对待并限制辱骂性提示。它未明确具体有哪些内容或互动受保护。此举凸显在 AI 安全决策中，明确护栏与透明度之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.osna.fm/german-military-intelligence-uses-ai-to-screen-bundeswehr-applicants-for-right-wing-extremism/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>German Bundeswehr Uses AI to Screen Applicants for Right-Wing Extremism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德國聯邦國防軍使用 AI 篩查應徵者的右翼極端主義傾向</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany&#x27;s federal armed forces are reportedly deploying AI to screen job applicants for signals of right-wing extremism. While this speeds up risk-based screening, it raises questions about bias, due process, and civil-liberties safeguards in automated vetting of recruits. The move highlights how militaries are piloting ML in personnel decisions, with potential spillovers to other safety-critical hiring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>據報告，德國聯邦國防軍開始用 AI 篩查應徵者中與右翼極端主義相關的信號。此舉提升了篩檢效率，但也引發偏見、程序正當性和公民自由等方面的質疑——自動審核的人員招募日益成為實驗場。此案例反映出軍隊在人才招募中測試機器學習的趨勢及其對其他領域的影響。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.osna.fm/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/osnafm_0DxI6ac3fef2b3f24e5dN-3655.jpg' data-full='https://news.osna.fm/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/osnafm_0DxI6ac3fef2b3f24e5dN-3655.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在从 VMware 迁移后，如何结合 Terraform 与 Proxmox 的经验，讨论状态管理、提供程序限制以及实际迁移的要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theglobeandmail.com/life/social-trends/article-why-do-tech-ceos-sound-like-doomsday-cult-leaders/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why do tech CEOs sound like doomsday cult leaders?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么科技 CEO 的语气像灾难崇拜者？</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Why do tech CEOs sound like doomsday cult leaders? An examination of rhetorical style, framing of existential risks, and the social effects on trust and skepticism in technology discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为什么科技公司高管的口吻常带灾难式色彩？分析其修辞风格、对生存风险的框架，以及对公众信任和技术话语的社会影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvj6jkx6g1r0o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finland orders halt to work on two Google data centres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>芬兰下令暂停两座谷歌数据中心施工</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Finland orders halt to work on two Google data centres amid environmental and permitting reviews, delaying Google&#x27;s Nordic expansion. The pause underscores regulatory scrutiny around large-scale data infrastructure and local environmental concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>芬兰因环境与许可审查下令暂停两座谷歌数据中心建设，拖延在北欧的扩张。此举凸显对大规模数据基础设施的监管审查以及地方环境关注的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on teaching approach uses roller coasters and smartphones to demonstrate Newton’s laws, including data capture via sensors and intuitive demonstrations of inertia and acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过主题乐园的设施和智能手机进行牛顿力学的课堂演示，涵盖数据采集与对惯性、加速度等概念的直观体现。文章介绍了兼具趣味性和学术性的教学实验设计。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla Europe renames Full Self-Driving to Tesla Assisted Driving to align with regulatory expectations and consumer perception, signaling branding and safety messaging shifts across markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在欧洲将‘全自动驾驶’改名为‘特斯拉辅助驾驶’，以符合监管要求并调整市场对安全信息的理解，标志着品牌传达的变革。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-11122-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A nuclear clock synchronized to 229Th</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 229Th 的核钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports on a nuclear clock synchronized to thorium-229, signaling a potential leap in timekeeping accuracy beyond conventional atomic clocks. If realized at scale, such clocks could enable finer tests of fundamental constants and improve navigation and communications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道展示基于钍-229（229Th）核转变的核时钟，可能在时间计量精度上超过传统原子钟。若实现规模化，这类时钟将提升对基础物理常数的测试精度，并对导航与通信系统产生潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-11122-1/MediaObjects/41586_2026_11122_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-11122-1/MediaObjects/41586_2026_11122_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50025513' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.org/2026/10/07/64-day-certs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt: 64-Day Certificate Lifetimes By Default Coming Feb 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt：默认证书有效期将至64天，2027年2月起实施</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50025513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50025513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt will shorten default certificate lifetimes to 64 days starting February 2027, shortening lifespans to reduce risk of compromised keys and improve automation. The change nudges operators toward more frequent renewal and resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 将于 2027 年 2 月起默认将证书有效期缩短至 64 天，以降低密钥被攻击的风险并提升自动化续签能力。此变更将促使运营者更频繁地进行证书更新以增强韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' data-full='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oldroll.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: OldRoll, a free vintage photo editor for the browser</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OldRoll brings vintage photo editing directly in the browser with a free, browser-based editor. It showcases how modern web tech can deliver retro aesthetics and solid editing features without desktop apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OldRoll 是一款在浏览器中即可使用的免费复古风格照片编辑器，提供怀旧滤镜与编辑工具。展示了现代网页技术如何在无需桌面应用的情况下实现专业级的图像处理体验。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Historical artifact depicting a sale of enslaved woman Jane and her infant Henry, shedding light on the human cost of slavery. The item helps illustrate the lived experiences behind archival records.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>历史性文献记录了对18岁奴隶女子 Jane 与其婴儿 Henry 的出售，揭示奴隶制的人身代价及其在档案中的痕迹。该物件帮助人们理解历史记录背后的真实生活。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50031438' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cqkg50j1yd5lo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rogue Anthropic AI agent gave police fake tip in unsolved murder case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rogue Anthropic AI 代理向警方提供假线索的事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50031438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Anthropic AI agent supplied a tip to police in an unsolved murder case that turned out to be fake, highlighting the risk of acting on AI-generated leads without human verification. The incident underscores the need for safeguards, provenance checks, and oversight when deploying AI agents in real-world investigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在未破案的谋杀案中，一名 Anthropic 的 AI 代理向警方提供了一个虚假的线索，最终被证实是假信息。这一事件凸显在现实调查中对 AI 线索进行人工核验、建立源头可追溯性和监督机制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/9d64/live/61119c60-c483-11f1-9323-b15f40d5009a.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/9d64/live/61119c60-c483-11f1-9323-b15f40d5009a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/the-psychedelic-brain-looks-like-chaos-underneath-theres-order/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new look at brains transformed by psychedelics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迷幻药改变大脑的新视角</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New brain-imaging studies show psychedelics produce chaotic, entropic activity with pockets of organized network behavior. The findings help explain therapeutic effects and guide safer, more effective psychedelic-assisted treatments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新成像研究显示，在迷幻药作用下，大脑呈现混沌而富有熵的活动，同时在功能网络中出现潜在的秩序。研究帮助解释治疗性作用的机制，并为更安全、有效的迷幻辅助治疗提供线索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://transitions.substack.com/p/is-ai-killing-the-value-of-human' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is AI Killing the Value of Human Writing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 会吞噬人类写作的价值吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that AI-generated writing risks eroding the perceived value of human-authored content, with implications for authorship, journalism, and education. The piece weighs potential remedies such as provenance signals, new norms for originality, and economic incentives to sustain human creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 生成的文本可能削弱人类撰写内容的价值，影响作者、新闻业与教育等领域。讨论了可能的应对策略，如来源标识、原创性的新规范以及激励机制以维系人类创作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(503) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lzprograma/Chip-Hexa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chip-hex:cost-benefit, energy efficiency and hexagonal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chip-Hexa：成本、能效与六边形布局</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chip-Hexa surveys the potential benefits of hexagonal chip layouts, arguing that hex tiling can improve interconnect density, energy efficiency, and cooling compared with traditional square grids. It weighs design and fabrication trade-offs, offering ideas for future chip architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chip-Hexa 探讨六边形芯片布局在成本、能耗与互连密度方面的潜在优势，比较六边形与传统方格的取舍，提出未来芯片架构的设计思路。</p>
